# Additions 2026-09-18 04:30 UTC — chat session (Claimocity feed outage + fix). No new AMD/Stedi calls; Postman unchanged.

## Claimocity feed — two paths (never confuse them)
- Charges into AMD: Claimocity DFT^P03 HL7 files on sftp.claimocity.io (account kykzfgie, /download) are picked up by the AMD integration, which clears each file. This path never stopped: the 9/17 3 PM ET run found 56 new HCA hospital visits (DOS 9/14–9/16) in Claim Inspector. Posting lags 1–3 days, daytime batches.
- Our Drive copies: Cloud Run Job `claimocity-pull` (GCP project [name]-50717, us-east1, service account claimocity-pull@[name]-50717.iam.gserviceaccount.com) runs every 30 min and copies new SFTP files into Drive "Claimocity drop" (1XskJ8BJAC-YQRLRFBZ9lWuyEJiU1-htI) as download__<name>.hl7, one per day about 07:30 UTC. It catches a file only because it runs before the AMD pickup clears it.

## Outage 9/10–9/17 and fix (9/18)
- Every execution since 9/10 died at the Drive login: `google.auth.exceptions.RefreshError: invalid_grant: Token has been expired or revoked` (exit 1). The deployed script used a copy of William's personal Google refresh token from the secret `drive-user-creds`; the token was revoked ~9/10.
- The Claimocity "Password reset" email (admin@claimocity.com, 9/9 10:47 PM ET) was a coincidence: portal/app login, not the SFTP account; SFTP login still works.
- Fix: William shared the Drive folder with the job's service account as Editor and redeployed main.py using `google.auth.default(scopes=[drive])` (code now in repo `claimocity/cloud_run/`). Execution claimocity-pull-j2hvb: `drive folder ok: 12 files already there` / `done: 0 copied, 4 skipped` — nothing newer than 9/9 remains on SFTP (already cleared by the AMD pickup). 9/10–9/17 copies are not recoverable from SFTP; AMD has the charges. Expect download__20260918... in the folder ~07:30Z 9/18 (a send_later check is armed for 09:00Z).
- Never re-run claimocity_redeploy_paste.txt (restores the expired-token version). claimocity_gcs_paste.txt = bucket alternative (ehpa-claimocity-drop, unused); claimocity-reader-key.json = read-only key for that bucket.

## Cloud Shell one-liners (William pastes; Claude sessions cannot reach GCP, and the session credential policy refuses using the reader key)
- describe: `gcloud run jobs describe claimocity-pull --region us-east1 --project [name]-50717 --format="yaml(spec.template.spec.template.spec.containers[0].env,spec.template.spec.template.spec.serviceAccountName)"`
- run: `gcloud run jobs execute claimocity-pull --region us-east1 --project [name]-50717 --wait`
- read THAT run's output in order: `gcloud logging read 'resource.type="cloud_run_job" AND labels."run.googleapis.com/execution_name"="claimocity-pull-<id>"' --order=asc --project [name]-50717 --format="value(textPayload)"` (without --order=asc the newest entry comes first and `tail` shows the oldest run).
- Reading it: `done: 0 copied, N skipped` = both logins fine, nothing new at Claimocity; `copied ...` = files landed; `UPLOAD FAILED ... 403` = switch to the bucket paste; `Authentication failed` = SFTP password changed -> new version of secret claimocity-sftp-pass. The audit line "failed to complete, 0/1 tasks were a success" has no reason in it.

## Facts for the routine
- The 2-hourly runs only list the Drive folder ("Facesheet folders ... Claimocity ... nothing new"); no run has ever read an HL7 file, and claimocity_in1.py is not in any prompt. Health = newest download__ file ≤ 2 days old (copy job) AND new hospital visits appearing in Claim Inspector (AMD path).
- Proposed prompt v8 line: the 9 AM ET run reports "Claimocity drop: newest file <date>" and flags it when older than 2 days.
- 9/17 Pam admit emails: 5 admits had no AMD chart yet ("Claimocity feed pending") — those charts appear only when the AMD path posts the encounters.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-19 0910 UTC (5am ET 9/19 backend billing run, ehpa cloud)

No new AMD call shapes. Behaviours learned this run (chart ids only), all on the Medicare Part B ERA check 819814877 ($46,350.13, received by AMD 02:03 AMD clock 9/19, deposit date 09/22/2026; AMD lists it as two ERA Center entries, 1051714 = claims 1-500 "819814877(0)" and 1051715 = claims 501-554 "819814877(1)"):

- **The ERA Center Post button posts the lines it accepts even when it answers a fault.** `posterapayments` on 1051714 (399 postable visits, $33,327.40) returned HTTP 200 with fault -2147217287 "N payments could not be posted ... or post each payment manually" (Error Counters PaymentEntry=N), and era_post logged "posted 0 visits" - but the next read of the charges (3 minutes later) showed 486 lines / $33,915.25 carrying a non-void payment with check number 819814877(0)/(1), and the check's unpostedamount had dropped from $46,350.13 to $12,434.88. The fault counts only the REFUSED lines; the rest post. era_post.py's ok/posted tally is therefore wrong after a partial fault - trust the register's 'already-posted' re-read and GET /api/era/checks unpostedamount.
- **era_fallback's "$0 open insurance balance = already adjudicated" gate is wrong when the charge carries NO non-zero payment.** 124 lines / $8,421.49 on this check landed on charges with insbal 0.00, patbal = full charge, paid 0.00 and only a $0 wrong-payer denial payment (BCBS check 213403769 9/9, UNI20 26007B1000256937 9/6, SUN01 0917650774 9/11): the $0 denial had transferred the whole charge to the PATIENT balance, then the re-routed claim was paid by Medicare. William's standing flag ("wrong-payer denials never stay on the patient balance") applies: `move_to_ins.move(cid)` (full-visit echo, no --seq = no rebill) returns the balance to insurance, then `manual_post.post(cid, "FLO02", pay, wo, "WOINS", checknumber, depositdate, note)` posts the Medicare line - helper `era_patbal_post_0919_0910.py` (--dry first). 83 lines / 74 visits / 6 charts qualified (patbal > 0, no non-zero payment); the 41 lines whose charge is at $0 on BOTH sides with no payment (write-off / WOMCAD reversal patterns, e.g. pat21442 99308 10/07/2025 with a WOMCAD write-off and a SUN01 $0 CO22 line) stay true residuals per William's 9/13 rule - reported, not posted.
- **A dismissed ERA check's lines can still be read**: `GET /api/era/checks/dismiss?DismissedDateBegin=2026-09-19` -> `[{id, dismissedat, paymentnumber, claimcount ...}]`; `POST /api/era/checks/reversedismiss` body `[{"id":1051716,"dismissedat":"2026-09-19T02:24:59.987"}]` -> `[{"issuccess":true,"id":1051716,"actionname":2}]`; then `GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid=1051716` returns the 5 claims again (it returns an empty eracheckdetails while the check is dismissed); `POST /api/era/checks/dismiss` "1051716" -> `{"issuccess":true,"id":1051716,"actionname":1}` re-dismisses. Used because era_clear dismissed the $0 denial check 1051716 (Medicare 349974158) before its lines were cached - cache before dismissing (era_step_b runs on the money list only).
- Medicare $0 denial check 349974158 (1051716): 5 claims, all CO-B9 "patient enrolled in hospice" with RARC MA01 - pat45668 99349 09/18/2025 $198, pat24799 99349 09/18/2025 $198, pat20670 99309 10/08/2025 $141, pat12182946 99487 08/22/2026 $206, pat17477267 99487 08/23/2026 $206 = $949 - hospice-period visits; GV/GW direction is William's (POYNOR rule) - board row, no rebill, no write-off.
- Orphan line on 1051714: claim 15439775, pat17477530 visit 19453715 99233 09/02/2026 paid $85.46, AMD error 5 "Procedure codes do not match" - the charge (23364598, billed FLO02 09/04/2026) is VOIDED, as are that chart's 09/03 and 09/04 99233 visits (19453774, 19453755), with no live replacement; the chart's live 08/24-09/01 visits are on CIG13 (Cigna HealthSpring MA, paid). Left in the Center (holds the check), listed for William.

## 2026-09-20 09:10 UTC - 5am ET 9/20 columns run (ehpa cloud Code Routine, run 2026-09-20T0910Z)

No new call shape this run. One RULE learned (Unbilled column):
- **Re-created visits keep the chart's default insorder.** The 18 Jan-Feb 2026 SNF visits the 9/10 Garland-G->Effinger profile swap re-created (ids 19454223-19454251, 16 charts) sat on Unbilled as 'A payment from the prior payor is required' with a BLANK carrier and no bill date. Cause: the new visit's `insorder` was the chart default (Medicare first), so every `chargerebill sequencenumber=<Medicaid seq>` (9/11, 9/16, 9/17) queued a SECONDARY claim that AMD holds until the first plan has a payment; `hideunbillablevisits` only hid the row and the refreshed snapshot re-listed it. Fix that works: `move_to_ins.move(cid, seq=<Medicaid seq>, rebill_seq=<Medicaid seq>)` (full-visit `updvisitwithcharges` echo with `insorder="<seq>"` only) then `freq1_visit.run(vid, cid, seq)` -> 204; the charge's queued claim now targets that plan as primary. Helper: `unbilled_fix_0920_0910.py` (dry then live; register `registers/unbilled_fix_2026-09-20T0910Z.json`, 18 fixed / 32 released / 1 held / 0 faults).
- Corollary for the 9/8 'prior payor' release rule: before releasing, split the rows - a row whose charge has NO carrier and NO bill date is not 'waiting on the primary', it is a mis-ordered visit that needs the insorder fix above; only rows whose primary was actually billed get the release.
- Unbilled snapshot behaviour: the cached list refreshed itself between the 0710Z run (55 rows) and 09:16Z (87 rows) with no claim run in between - AMD re-evaluates released rows on its own schedule, not only on claim runs.

## captured_calls additions - 2026-09-21 06:00 UTC (chat session, William live, 9/21 ~1:00-2:00 AM ET) - NO new call shapes (vocabulary unchanged, Postman unchanged)

### Crossover marker in AMD data (proven 9/21 on AET12, AAR01, BCBS, MCAD, WEL04 charges)
- On `getchargedetaildataicd10` the ERA-posted primary payment carries `status="C"` AND the Insurance Billing History (`<insbill>`) holds an entry for the SECONDARY carrier with `paperedi="X"`, `claimrun="N/A"`, `IsAMDSystem="0"`, `billeddate` = the primary posting date. That pair = the primary remit reported the claim forwarded to the next payer (835 claim status 19 / MA18) and AMD marked the secondary as billed by crossover. Controls: primary payment `status="B"` (or "H" on old paymenttype-10 postings) has NO X entry and AMD generates an electronic (E) secondary itself the same/next day (Florida Blue pat17477267 run 1700186; Medicaid [member id]/pat17470743; WellCare pat17469446). AARP Medigap (AAR01) charges paid by Medicare 9/2-9/19/2026 all show C + X and no E.
- RULE: never force-rebill a secondary that carries an X entry - the payer already holds the crossover claim; the balance waits on that payer's remit. A freq-1 rebill of an X-marked secondary is what produced the nine AET12 transmissions (runs 1700151 -> 1700202, claim letters B-K).

### AET12 (Aetna Senior Supplemental, car42009) - state 9/21 06:00 UTC

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-21 05:30-06:25 UTC chat (William live, ~1:30-2:30 AM ET) - payment reason codes

## What was wrong
54 Unbilled rows 'Payment reason codes are missing or do not balance' ($11,858.58; 37 at the visit level + 17 under 'One or more charges within this visit have errors'): secondaries (CON08 13, HUM01 12, MCAD 8, SUN01 7, BCBS 7, TRI05 4, UNI20 3) stuck behind Medicare primaries that the ERA fallback had hand-posted with `manual_post.post` (plain addpayments) 9/13-9/19 - 43 on the two held Medicare entries 1051714/1051715 (ERA 819814877), 11 on eight 2024 checks dismissed 9/13 (892988 893080 895008 893862 893899 893939 894075 895373). A plain addpayments carries no `<payreason>` children (9/12 probe), so AMD cannot build the CAS segments of the next payer's claim.

## The fix that works (no UI capture needed)
1. `gettxhistory` -> the visit's live payments with no `payreason` children (amounts are negative there; `insbal`/`patbal`/`fee` on the charge).
2. `void` class=void the code-less payment, the WOINS posted with it (same `createtime`), and any $0 code-less line (9/12 rule).
3. The ERA claim behind it: the fallback register (checkid/checkclaimid/chargeid), else the payment's check number against GET /api/era/checks and GET /api/era/checks/dismiss?DismissedDateBegin=. A dismissed check: POST /api/era/checks/reversedismiss `[{"id","dismissedat"}]` -> GET checkdetails (claims come back with their `carc` ids) -> POST dismiss again (the 9/19 recipe). A processed check answers nothing - its lines come from `era_details/<checkid>.json` written earlier.
4. Re-post the line with the **ERA-linked** addpayments: `postingmethod="era" eraclaimid=<checkclaimid> erareportid=<checkid> checknumber=<paymentnumber>` and the remit's `<reasonlist payment allowed><reason id=<reportreasondetailid> reasonid=<payreasonid> code name amount units="0" group/>` (the 9/3 Pinckney shape). Reply `<Results success="1" paymentid="NNN"/>`; gettxhistory then shows the payment WITH `payreason` children (CO45 / PR2 / CO253). Amounts: pay = remit paid (capped at the open insurance balance), wo = CO/OA/PI adjustments capped so pay+wo <= balance (WOINS; WOLATE CO-29; WONCS CO-96/204/50); PR left on the visit -> the secondary gets it. Works whether the check is listed, dismissed or processed.
5. Verify: on every live payment of the visit |paid| + sum(reason amounts) == fee. Then `hideunbillablevisits hide="-1"` with the **numeric** visit id (`vst19420307` faults: "Conversion failed when converting the nvarchar value ... to data type int").
Helper: payreasons.py (`--list`, `--unbilled --live`, `--visit`, `--repost-file`, `--audit --since-payid`). era_fallback_0913b.py now posts through `payreasons.post_line` - manual_post.post is never used for an ERA line again.

## The sweep (new AMD behaviour - the rule that came out of tonight)
The first 20 visits were done with the Post button (posterapayments, FORCE_VISITS = one visit, after voiding that visit's hand post) and came out right. But while single visits were being posted, AMD's engine went on and posted 29 OTHER claims of check 1051714 (ids 24653594-24653668, 05:52-05:56 UTC, in check order) - each capped at the visit's open balance, i.e. the leftover PR2 ($17.50 posted for a $68.62 line) with the full reason list - on visits that still carried their hand posts; then both entries were marked processed and dismissed (22:59 AMD clock, changedby POI0712). Voiding a capped payment does NOT re-open the ERA claim (reversedismiss brought the check back with its 76 never-posted zero-balance claims, not those). All 29 were voided and re-posted through the ERA-linked addpayments from the cache. RULE: never Post a single visit of a check while other claims of that check sit on hand-posted / partially-open visits; void those first, or post single lines with payreasons.post_line; after any Post, `payreasons.py --audit --since-payid <last id before the post>` and void + re-post anything capped.

## Result
54/54 visits repaired and released 05:45-06:20 UTC: 20 via the Post button (correct), 34 via the ERA-linked addpayments (23 after the sweep, 11 old 'Procedure codes do not match' lines). Final audit over the 15 charts of 819814877: 58 live payments created tonight, every one equal to the remit's paid amount, no duplicates. Unbilled 55 -> 1 (pat17470743 MCAD $16.93 prior-payor row, a different case). ERA Center: 0 unposted checks (1051714/1051715 processed + dismissed; the eight 2024 checks restored and dismissed again). Registers: registers/payreason_repair_2026-09-21T*.json, capped_fix_voids_2026-09-21T0605Z.json; run folder nightly/runs/2026-09-21T0545Z-chat (probe script, audit, before-list).

## Calls
- addpayments ERA-linked with reasonlist -> vocabulary payments-era/addpayments-era-linked-manual-post-with-reason-codes (proven; keeps reasons).
- recipe entry payments-era/repair-payment-reason-codes-unbilled.
- hideunbillablevisits: numeric visit id (vocabulary entry fixed).
- posterapayments: sweep rule appended; reversedismiss: recipe appended.

## 2026-09-22 chat — referring provider on re-rendered visits (CO208/N286)
- The referring provider (Box 17 / 2310A) comes from the visit's referral plan (`refplan`), not the visit. Main-plan `refprov` ids mirror the rendering profiles: rprov13 = Effinger, rprov15 = Garland-G, rprov14 = third provider (survey of 37 charts; the 9/19 nightly already used rprov13 for an Effinger patient). getdemographic `<refplan>` carries no name attr; no referring-provider list action exists (4 names probed, all faults - not in the registry, do not guess further).
- Root cause of check 1051726 CO208/N286 x9 ($1,269): the 9/11 re-render kept each visit's main plan, so claims went out rendering=Effinger / referring=Garland-G (terminated Medicaid ID). Fix (helper `billing/rebill/fix_referring_provider.py`): addreferralplan ismain=0 status=stat1 refprov=rprov13 facility=<main plan's> -> new rplan (worked on all 9 charts, no get-or-return quirk) -> full-charge resave with refplan=new (no preauth attr) -> verify refplan + charge count -> freq-1 + chargerebill on Medicaid's own sequence. 10 charts, 9 new plans, 34 visits repointed (charge counts unchanged), the 9 denied rebilled (all 204). Register: practices/ehpa/registers/fix_refprov_2026-09-22.log.
- Standing rule: any visit re-rendered from prof15 to prof13 must also be repointed off an rprov15 referral plan, or Medicaid denies N286.

## 2026-09-22 chat (cont.) — changing a chart's DEFAULT referring provider on the back end
- `addreferralplan` with `ismain="1"` (status=stat1, sequence=1, refprov=rprov13, facility=<old main's>) CREATES a new main plan and DEMOTES the existing main to ismain=0 (pat44976: rplan18462131 main rprov13, rplan45133 -> ismain 0). Even the ismain="0" plans created earlier became main. This is the captured way to change the default referring provider — no plan update action exists and no UI step is needed. All 10 N286 charts now default to rprov13 (Effinger).
- Rebill sequence rule: rebill on the sequence the VISIT is billed to (lastbilledcarrier / insorder); pushing a United/Sunshine-billed visit at the Medicaid FFS sequence returns HTTP 500. 47 charges resubmitted 9/22 (9 denied + 18 Medicaid-seq + 20 own-seq).
- Standing step: `billing/rebill/sweep_refprov.py` (nightly) — every open-balance chart whose main plan is rprov15 with open prof13 visits gets the ismain=1 plan + full-charge repoint + freq-1 rebill on own sequence. --dry lists them.

## 2026-09-22 chat — Medicaid/SMMC Garland-G -> Effinger, full past-year set (William)
- Runner `billing/rebill/medicaid_garland-g_fix.py` (targets from masswriteoffsearch per Medicaid carrier id, DOS >= 1 year): A = prof15 visits -> profile swap to prof13 with refplan=rprov13 main plan in the same resave (AMD voids + recreates on a new visit; recreations start insbilled=0 = already queued); B = prof13 visits on rprov15 charts -> refplan repoint; both freq-1 + chargerebill on the balance-holding plan's sequence. FCC (FLO99) balances route to MCAD FFS when car6113 is on file and no active SMMC plan, to the SMMC plan when one exists, else kept on FLO99 and flagged.
- Lessons: (1) AMD's server clock is ET and can be a calendar day behind the container (UTC) - never match createtime on the container date alone; (2) charge DETAIL reports proccode as pcodeNNNN while gettxhistory reports the CPT - match recreations on FEE + visit DATE, never on proccode, and keep a consumed set so two same-fee visits swapped back-to-back cannot claim one recreation; (3) settling gate matters at scale: 231 of 302 Garland-G-rendered Medicaid visits already carried a posted payment and were NOT re-rendered (that remainder is the WOMCAD/WOCAID2ND write-off case).
- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). AMD carrier `RET01` = `car6080` RETIRED RAILROAD MEDICARE (Palmetto GBA) takes the same MBI. Calls used: getclaimcenteroverview, getreviewcharges, getclaiminspectorbatches/getclaiminspectorcharges, getclaimunbillablecharges, getclaimrunstatus/getclaimexclusioncharges, getdemographic, gettxhistory (txh.fetch + pagenumber 2), GET /api/era/checks, GET /api/era/checkdetails, POST /api/era/locks/release-expired, POST /api/era/checks/dismiss. Learned behaviour (chart ids only):

- **gettxhistory is paged even with typefilter=0 (the txh.fetch shape).** pat17470743 (195 live charges): pagenumber=1 returned 102 charges (2024-2026), pagenumber=2 the other 93 (2023-2024), pagenumber=3 empty. `txh.fetch` reads page 1 only, so on a chart with more than ~100 charges the OLDEST visits are invisible to every helper built on it (era_clear.recorded, the ERA fallback's 'already posted' check, precheck anchors). Read page 2+ (same attrs, `pagenumber` incremented) until a page comes back empty when an ERA/denial line's visit is missing from page 1.
- **A $0 payer check whose every line sits on a charge already at $0 needs no cascade work** - WEL04 1051731 (Centene, 19 claims): 15 CO-18 duplicates were the 9/11/2026 freq-1 rebill of 2023 hospital visits WellCare paid 3/10/2024 (residuals written off WOMCAD 9/22/2026 by the chat batch); 4 CO-29 and the CO-96/CO-4 CCM lines were written off WOINS 9/6/2026; the CO-23/OA-23 secondary line already carried WellCare's 9/15 payment + WOCAID2ND. Recorded per line in registers/era_denials_1051731_2026-09-22T1310Z.json, check dismissed by era_clear (its recorded() gate passes on the non-void payments). Rebilling a visit the payer already adjudicated is exactly what draws CO-18 - the register keeps these visit ids so no later pass re-sends them.

- HCA inspector batch 127765 (19 visits, 17 charts): admission dates from the most recent 99223 within 45 days (16), the visit's own 99223 (2), or the stay's earlier visit that already carried the date (1); three Z96.6x-led charts (status-post joint replacement, secondary-only Z) took the add-I10 cascade before the resave; Pattern 11256 (S72.90XD) bypassed and logged x3; Source-of-Payment advisory bypassed x1.
- DECEASED GUARD (William 9/22): deceased_guard.check ran on every visit before its resave; the guard's Stedi lookup now sends NO Authorization header when secrets/stedi_live.key is absent (the ehpa cloud proxy attaches the credential) - it had returned 'unverified' on every FLO02 chart before this patch.
- UHC 'SUBSCRIBER AND SUBSCRIBER ID NOT FOUND' + 'PA MEMBER PICK REJECT' on a Medicare-secondary residual (pat17471365, claim run 1700216): the chart's seq-2 plan was an AARP Medicare Supplement Plan F entered under UNI20 (CPID 3429 = UHC commercial/MA). AMD 271 on the plan names 'AARP SUPPLEMENTAL PLANS INS BY UNITEDHEALTHCARE'; Stedi AARP 36273 with the same member id = ACTIVE; UHC 87726 = empty. Fix: AAR01 (car6600) added seq 3 with the same member id (no group), UNI20 end-dated the day before the first rejected DOS, both visits insorder 1,3 + freq1 rebilled on seq 3, exclusions completed. Note: AMD's Medicare 271 on this chart answers AAA 71 (birth date does not match) and the Stedi MBI lookup by name/DOB misses (ERR 72) although Medicare paid the primaries - the chart DOB may differ from CMS; flagged in the report.
- gettxhistory with typefilter=1 returns only charges with an open balance; pagenumber 2..4 repeat page 1 (no paging with that filter).

# captured_calls additions — 9/22/2026 chat session (paper & virtual-card remittances → ERA Center)

Merge into `captured_calls.md` (Payments & ERA). Chart ids as patNNN only.

## POST /api/era/upload/835 — proven with synthetic 835s (9/22/2026)
Captured 9/2/2026 from William's UI import (`era_upload.upload`). This session proved what the importer does with files WE build from paper EOBs (`billing/paper_remits/build835.py`):
- multipart field `file`, filename `*.835`, text/plain; response `{"issuccess":true,"filename","filesize","filemd5checksum"}`. The check shows in `GET /api/era/checks` asynchronously — 5 s to ~4.5 min on 9/22; polling every 5 s drew HTTP 500 from `/api/era/checks`, 10 s is fine.
- Dedupe is by file MD5, not TRN: identical bytes are skipped silently; a different file with the same TRN02 creates a NEW check (so a corrected re-upload is possible; the engine dismisses the old one). A CLP03 (charge) of 0 makes the importer drop the whole file silently — a summary-only claim gets CLP03 = paid.
- Matching: CLP01 = the AMD claim number printed on every payer's EOB as "Patient Acct #" (8-digit visit id + sequence letter, e.g. 19442529A) → the visit; each SVC line → the charge by CPT (a claim with no SVC lines still matches the visit and returns chargecount / chargecode / insurancebalance with error "Procedure codes do not match", chargedetailid −1 — a free probe; an EOB CPT that differs from the chart errors the same way → re-file with AMD's own CPT under TRN <ref>L / <ref>N, posted with the clean check number).
- Reasons: ONE reason per CAS segment. `CAS*OA*253*0.93*23*45.44` lost the 23 → "Contains invalid payment reason codes"; `CAS*OA*253*0.93` + `CAS*OA*23*45.44` reads both.
- AMT*B6 is ignored (allowed = charge − adjustments). BPR16 → depositdate; TRN02 → paymentnumber; N1*PR name/REF*2U → `systemcarrier` only when AMD recognizes it, otherwise the visit's billed carrier (MCAD/FLO02/BAN07 seen) — so the posting call sets the carrier explicitly.
- Posting = the ERA-linked addpayments (`payreasons.era_linked_xml`) with `carrierid="car<payer's plan on the chart>"`, `checknumber=<clean check number>`, `paymethod="11"` (CHK) and the reasonlist from checkdetails; then `POST /api/era/checks/dismiss`. Never the Post button on these checks (it would post under the billed carrier).

## Chart writes used (all previously captured shapes)
- `getdemographic` → the payer's plan on the chart; `addinsurance` (no group number) when missing — UNI01 car42005 (United American), BAN07 car41999 (Washington National/Bankers Life), AET12 car42009, MUT01 car42012, PHY10 car302810 (Physicians Life via ECHO), SIM01 car42019, FLO99 car42008, USAA car303211.
- `gettxhistory` before and after every post (live insbalance, payments with reason children, write-offs). NEW: `gettxhistory` with `visitid=<vid>` returns ONE visit's history — page 1 of a long chart misses old visits (`txh.fetch_visit`).
- `void` class=void on a write-off ONLY when its amount equals what the payer then paid (a premature WOINS); `move_to_ins.move(cid)` + `manual_post.post(cid, "FLO02", 0, amt, "WOINS")` re-applied two Medicare contractuals the first version of the engine had voided (pat45520 99308 $49.97, pat17473010 99223 $152.86) — recipe in rules.md.
- Overpayment seen: pat17473010 v19449180 99223 — Simply (7707158635) and Washington National (1942731109) each paid the $31.94 coinsurance; Simply's post kept, WN's voided (`void` class=void payment) and HELD for William.
- Charges voided by the 9/21 re-render and re-entered on NEW visits (pat21489 v19445157 → v19455360; pat23161 v19450698 → v19455341): the FCC payments were filed against the new visits' claim numbers.

## Numbers
13 ScanSnap batches (Gmail attachments; 8 fetched through Zapier `gmail_get_attachment_by_filename` because the Gmail connector's RAW read fails above ~6 MB), 502 pages OCR'd; 40 (payer, check/card) groups, 89 claims $3,289.03 on the EOBs; POSTED 95 lines $3,046.70 + $1,795.97 write-offs (register: Drive `billing agent project files/paper remits 2026-09-22`). Holds by design: ~204 FCC $0 COB statement claims, 190 Florida Blue paper-RA claims (EFT, already electronic), 7 claims with no readable check reference, 2 unreadable account numbers; 1 HOLD (the overpayment above).

## practices/ehpa/rules.md lines (already in the bundle; repeated here for a session that merges by hand)
- (9/22/2026, chat) PAPER / VIRTUAL-CARD REMITS are posted through the ERA Center, never by hand (module billing/paper_remits; docs/input_channels.md).
- (9/22/2026, chat) Never void every write-off on a line to make room for a paper payment; only the premature one. Medigap + Medicaid both paying the same coinsurance = OVERPAYMENT HOLD.
- (9/22/2026, chat) Medicaid-plan paper payments carry their residual write-off in the SAME post (WOCAID2ND / WOMCAD).
- (9/22/2026, chat) Zelis virtual cards are keyed in Square's Virtual Terminal by hand; an unrun card is re-issued as a paper check → dedupe claims across mailings on (payer family, acct, paid).
- (9/22/2026, chat) Simply / USAA / Aetna SSI / United American / MoO / Physicians Life statements arrive on paper only; ScanSnap cloud app → Gmail/Drive is their input channel (Gmail RAW > 6 MB needs Zapier).

- LESSON (PDTP0303 on secondary claims): the 9/22 chat session's ERA-linked payment posts made AMD queue the next-payer claims of ~100 hospital visits; every one left on claim runs 1700227/1700228/1700229 (9/22 21:06 AMD clock) WITHOUT an admission date - the visits' extins records (read back with getchargedetaildataicd10 by charge id) carried no hospitalizationfrom attribute at all - and Change Healthcare rejected them 'CH 72 PDTP0303 MISSING RELATED ADMISSION DATE'. Repair that works without a full-visit resave (safe on visits with posted payments): updextrainsinfo per CPT echoing the existing extins attributes + hospitalizationfrom (own 99223 / most recent 99223 within 45 days from gettxhistory pages 1-4) + resubmissioncode 1 / origrefnum blank (resave.clean_extins drops blank/1900 dates), then POST /api/claim/chargerebill with sequencenumber = the charge's lastbilledcarrier (the sequence the rejected claim went to) -> 204; readback shows hospitalizationfrom on the extins and insbilled 0. 101 visits repaired this way, 0 faults (excl_0923_0510.py).
- LESSON (Claim Inspector PENDING batch): 19 nursing-home visits created by the Claimocity feed at 9/21 22:05 sat in batch '1' as claimstatus PENDING (claimstatusid 10) with errorcount 0 and did NOT leave on the 9/22 claim run; a full-visit resave (updvisitwithnewcharges, force=1, inspectorenabled=1, onhcfa=1) re-runs the inspector - the 9/13 Orme lesson holds for feed visits too.
- LESSON (address PATCH): on a chart whose email field holds a phone number the Edit-address PATCH answers 500 'Email is not a valid format.' - addr_line1_fix clears the email first, then the line-1 PATCH answers 200 (pat17477828); a chart with a phone on file and an empty email saves first time (pat17477827, pat17477830, pat17477183).
- DATA (Stedi): a Florida Medicaid 271 that lists 'SMMC MMA Capitated <plan>' means the MMA plan (SUN01 with the Medicaid number) bills first and Medicaid FFS is only the chart's second line (pat17477829, order 2,1); a GA-address Medicare-age patient answered ERR 72 on the MBI lookup for both FL and GA but insurance discovery (state GA) returned the UHC C-SNP with the plan dates (pat17477828).

# captured_calls additions — 2026-09-23 chat session (paper EOB sweep, part 2: Simply / FCC / CHAMPVA / Florida Blue / WellCare / UHC)

No new call SHAPES (every call below is already in the registry / vocabulary); what is new is what the tenant does with them.

## Posting (ERA Center, synthetic 835s — payments-era/upload-835 + recipe-paper-remit-to-era-center)
- 9 Simply claims the parser had missed on continuation pages (one chart, 99232 x 9) re-filed under TRN `<ref>S` and posted: $493.29. A re-filed group's SECOND file (the "AMD's own CPTs" retry) must carry the group's suffix in its TRN too (`<ref><suffix>L`), otherwise `find_check()` restores the earlier dismissed `<ref>L` check instead of waiting for the new upload.
- `done_acct()`: a plan claim with NO EOB lines counts as posted once any of its AMD lines carry a POSTED register row — the old `done_key(cpt=None)` re-uploaded such claims on every re-run.
- Two FCC paper-check claims were hidden in the "$0" statements by OCR (a paid total read as "$4.81"; a visit voided by the 9/21 re-render and re-entered as a new visit): $54.81 + $70.62 posted (the second on the re-entered visit, found with a chart-wide gettxhistory by DOS/CPT).
- CHAMPVA (CHA03): pays by EFT with an "information only" paper EOB and NO 835 ever reaches the ERA Center — $430.17 across six EOBs (2024 and 2026 dates of service) was unposted; some coinsurance had been transferred to the patient. Posted through the ERA Center from the paper EOBs (family CHAMPVA, ref = the EFT trace printed on the EOB, per-line paid = the open Medicare coinsurance, OA-23 = Medicare's payment, CO-45 = the rest so the engine's WOINS rule adjusts a contractual never written off). The CHA03 plan's member id is the beneficiary SSN as the existing CHA03 plans carry it. Patient balances were moved back first (`move_to_ins.move(cid)` without a sequence = move only, no rebill).
- Totals for the whole paper pile: 130 lines, $4,139.68 payments + $2,529.53 write-offs, ERA Center empty after every run.

## Reading the $0 statements against the live chart (gettxhistory visitid= + lookuppatient "LAST,FIR%")
- `lookuppatient` answers with `<patient id name chart ssn dob ...>`; `ET.fromstring` needs the bytes (latin-1) — the ISO-8859-1 declaration in the text response raises on str input. Two-word surnames need "LAST LAST2,FIR%"; comma names ("LAST,FIRST") are already in AMD's form.
- Duplicate charts are common (one patient with 15 charts, others with 2-4, same DOB) — probably the census feed creating a chart per admission. A name lookup must try every chart before declaring a visit missing.
- Florida Blue paper RAs (61 pages, 226 claims; claim numbers Q…/M…/F… + 8-digit AMD acct; columns charges / allowed / other-carrier / copay / coins / deductible / pt resp / paid; remark legend per RA): MEMNC = member not covered on the DOS; OCINF / OCMSP / EOMB2 / RVND* = FB is SECONDARY to Medicare and wants Medicare's adjudication; OCPMA / PR952 = paid as secondary; NC275 = ACP not a benefit (FEP); DN331 = no patient liability after Medicare; PR741/OB600 = federal retiree not in Part B paid at the Medicare fee schedule.
- On the State Employees' PPO / FB-retiree charts the plans sat as BCBS seq 1 / FLO02 seq 2: FB was billed first, answered $0, and AMD moved the $0 denial to the PATIENT balance. `charge@carrier` + `charge@billdate` (gettxhistory) show where each claim last went — the routine had already re-sent 16 of these visits to Medicare on 9/12-9/16, so only the 11 still last-billed to BCBS were re-sent (never a duplicate).
- FCC $0 statements: 041 primary paid >= Medicaid allowed; 14/075 no fee-schedule line (CCM 99487/99489, AWV G0439, ACP 99497); 76 "provider validation state requirements" only on Garland-G's Medicare-crossover NF claims (her FCC-primary claims pay). A paper Medicare RA whose pages read PCIP/HSIP/HPSA is the quarterly HPSA bonus remittance (10 % on assigned claims, $1,218.28 on 7/14/2026) — an EFT with no claim lines.

## William's word 9/23 ("rebill these to Medicare … write off the ones you recommend") — executed with the proven recipe
- Per visit still last-billed to BCBS: `freq1_visit.run(vid, cid, seq)` (frequency 1; its own rebill answers 500 while the balance is still on the patient — expected) then `move_to_ins.move(cid, "<medicare seq>,<fb seq>", rebill_seq=<medicare seq>)` → balance back to insurance, visit insorder Medicare-first, `chargerebill` 204. 11 visits $2,231.00.
- Patient default order: `ins_update.update(pid, <FLO02 insid>, sequence="2,1")` only when `<patient insorder>` does not already start with the Medicare sequence (two charts already were; one flipped 1,2 → 2,1).
- Claims already at Medicare with the balance still on the patient: `move_to_ins.move(cid)` only. 6 visits $1,636.00.
- Write-offs through `manual_post.post(cid, carrier, 0.0, amt, code, checknumber="PAPER0923", depositdate=today, note=…)` after a fresh live read of the line (never over-apply): WOCAID2ND 54 lines $833.85 under FLO99 (FCC members whose chart lacked the FCC plan got it via `add_ins.add(pid, "car42008", <FCC member id>)` first), WONCS 9 lines $1,510.00 (CCM/AWV/ACP on FCC-primary members; a FEP 99497). Lines whose chart shows a Medigap / Florida Blue secondary, a Humana-first order, or a Medicare $0 were HELD (22 lines $514.46) — not the payer's coinsurance.

## Mirror (William 9/23): GitHub inbox
- When the push is blocked the session leaves a PACKAGE (thin bundle .b64 + manifest with sha256) in Drive `github mirror` and the project doc `mirror/outbox/…`; the 2-hourly routine's GITHUB INBOX step applies it with `tools/mirror/apply_bundle.py` (sha256 + `git bundle verify`, no force), pushes main + the synced branch, regenerates Postman and refreshes Drive, and records it in `practices/ehpa/registers/bundles_applied.json`. docs/MIRROR.md.

## practices/ehpa/rules.md lines (already appended in this package)
See the five 9/23/2026 lines at the end of practices/ehpa/rules.md (package rule, CHAMPVA from paper, Florida Blue secondary / payer order, FCC $0 codes, HPSA bonus RA).

## 2026-09-23 09:10 UTC - 5am ET 9/23 columns run (ehpa cloud Code Routine, run 2026-09-23T0910Z)
- No new call shape this run. Columns 0/17/13/2 -> 0/16/13/2; ERA Center 1 check $33.86 -> 0 (Humana 1052806 posted by the Post button, dismissed by era_clear).
- Lesson (Claim Inspector Pattern 15775 'add-on 99489 without a primary'): `gettxhistory` typefilter="1" hides a VOIDED primary - pat17474541 v19430630 looked like a lone 99489 until typefilter="0" showed the 99487 (charge 23336997) with void="1" (billdate 01/31/2026). A 15775 hold on a CCM visit is a void question for William (restore_charge.py on his OK), never a bypass and never a resave of the surviving add-on alone.
- Lesson (ERA denial-only line): a Humana CO-185/N767 line can arrive on a chart that has NO Humana plan (pat17477607 v19453346: Medicare + United American Medigap) - a Medicare crossover Humana does not cover. Read the live charge carrier/billdate first: the balance was already at the Medigap secondary (UNI01, billed 9/16), so the rules.md 9/20 'CO185 -> WOINS' rule (Humana-secondary charts) does not apply; record only, no write-off, no rebill.

# captured_calls additions — 2026-09-23 chat (wrong-payer / Medicare-first sweep, Stedi verified)

## Stedi calls used at scale (all proven 9/23; key = secrets/stedi_live.key from the "stedi claude key" Gmail thread — a chat container's proxy does NOT attach the credential, the file must exist)
- `stedi.mbi(first, last, yyyymmdd, "FL", dos)` (MBILUNOSSN) ~250 calls: MBI + Part A/B plan dates + the Advantage plan as a `U` entity (type HN/PR/PS, STC 30+CQ, 'Primary Payer' name, coordinationOfBenefits date) + Part D as `R OT` STC 88 + QMB as `R QM` + `planDateInformation.dateOfDeath`. ERR 42 "Unable to Respond at Current Time" = CMS outage (retry later, 6 of 246 the first pass); ERR 72/73 = name/DOB miss (JR/SR suffixes, hyphens, two-word surnames) — 80 of 246, most of them settled by AMD's 271 on the billed plan (ACTIVE = right payer).
- `stedi.elig("CMS", first, last, dob, <chart MBI>, dos)` — the CMS route with an MBI in hand: bypasses the name match; the MSG segment carries "Railroad Retirement Medicare Beneficiary" for RRB beneficiaries (pat12182505).
- `stedi.elig("BCBSF", ..., member, dos)` / `("87726", ...)` / `("77027", ...)` on the chart member id with the DOS: FB returns planStatus + planDateInformation (periodStart/End, planBegin/End) — an FB 'active' can be a BlueMedicare product (rules.md); UHC returns the member id and plan name (an MBI passed as the member id also resolves — pat17472228 -> 9-digit UHC id); Florida Medicaid returns the coverage class ("FP | Limited to family planning services" = not a physician payer).
- `stedi.disc(first, last, dob, "FL", dos)` ($1.50, 17 calls): items[] with payer name, memberId, planDateInformation, benefitsInformation; found CMS (with MBI), UHC Dual Complete / AARP MA ids with plan windows, Simply MMA, Florida Blue BlueOptions, WellCare PDPs (= Medicare exists), family-planning Medicaid; "0 found" = uninsured (b. 1996, b. 1982 with FP-Medicaid only).

## AMD calls (no new shapes; behaviours learned)
- `ins_update.update(pid, insid, sequence="3,1")` sets the CHART default insurance order (proven again x40); an END-DATED plan cannot lead it (AMD answers insorder=""), so add the plan first, set the order, end-date last. `ins_update.update(pid, insid, enddate="mm/dd/yyyy")` end-dates in place. Concurrency fault -2147217398 "Another user has changed this insurance record" appears when another process (the sweep's getdemographic reads) touches the chart in the same second — retry.
- `add_ins.add(pid, car, member, begindate)` with begindate = Part B start (from the CMS record) — 16 plans added (8 FLO02, 3 UHCMA car302853, 2 AAR01 car6600, UNI20 car6404, BCBS car6062, SIM01 car42019).
- `freq1_visit.run(vid, cid, seq)` answers "rebill seq N -> 500" on a visit whose balance/insorder is not yet on that plan; the following `move_to_ins.move(cid, order, rebill_seq=seq)` sets the visit insorder and its rebill answers 204 — that pair is the rebill recipe (118/118 -> 204).
- `updvisitwithcharges` with insportion=0/patportion=fee on a charge that carries insurance payments UNAPPLIES them (repeat of the 9/21 lesson): the payments land in `unappliedpaymentlist` (negative amounts, `actvpaymentid`), the write-offs are voided. Repair used 9/23 on 9 charges: `move_to_ins.move(cid)` (portions back), `void_tx.void_unapplied(actvpaymentid)` per credit, `manual_post.post(cid, "FLO02", pay, contractual, "WOINS", checknumber=<original>, depositdate=<original>)` — AMD then moves the coinsurance to the patient itself (no next payer on the visit).
- getchargedetail `allowed` becomes the paid amount after a manual addpayments; a $0 addpayments (pay 0, wo 0) is accepted and harmless.
- `lookuppatient` (class lookup, name="LAST,FIR%") finds hyphenated surnames only by the first token ("POULTER,G%"), and a patient can have two charts (pat17470713 / pat17472439).

## Where the code and registers are
- GitHub main (package `effinger-billing-backend_2026-09-23_payer-sweep`, sha256 cdfadf70…, in the project's GITHUB INBOX / Drive github mirror manifest): `billing/payer_sweep/{medfirst_sweep.py, cascade_act_0923.py, medfirst_act_0923.py, medfirst_act2_0923.py, medfirst_report.py, README.md}`; `docs/captured_calls_additions_2026-09-23_payer-sweep.md` (this file); `practices/ehpa/rules.md` 9/23 block.
- Drive `billing agent project files/wrong payer sweep 2026-09-23`: sweep_rebilled, sweep_verification, sweep_your_decisions, sweep_summary, sweep_act_registers, medfirst_notes (Google Sheets; PHI-OK working copy). The workbook wrong_spayer_sweep_2026-09-23.xlsx went to William in chat.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-23 1910 UTC (3 PM ET 9/23 backend billing run, ehpa cloud Code Routine, run 2026-09-23T1910Z)

No new AMD or Stedi call SHAPES this run. Behaviours learned (all with proven calls from the registry):

- **Florida Blue 835s through the ERA Center Post button (posterapayments) leave the CO-45 on the PATIENT balance.** Five Florida Blue checks (trace 423802576, 2609231016, 2609230762, 2609230531, 213436603; carrier 6062) posted 35 money lines ($2,002.65). Readback of every posted charge (gettxhistory visitid=): the payment carries `<payreason code="CO45" amount=...>` but the charge shows `patbal = fee - paid`, `insbal 0` - the carrier's ERA details carry `payreasonwoamount 0` / `chargeequalswriteoff` false, so AMD records the contractual as a reason only and transfers the remainder to the patient. The 9/14 Availity hand posts behaved identically (row bcbs-patient-balance-audit-0918). Correction = the captured 9/18 recipe, now scripted as `era_fb_fix_1910.py` part A: `move_to_ins.move(cid)` (no seq = no rebill) then `manual_post.post(cid, "BCBS", 0.0, patbal - PR amounts, "WOINS", checknumber=<trace>, depositdate=<check date>)`; AMD auto-transfers the PR-1/PR-2 part back to the patient (verified: 99232 fee 111, paid 34, PR-2 34 -> after WOINS 43.00 the patient balance reads 34.00). 32 lines / $2,221.87 corrected; 3 lines already at patient 0. Until the Florida Blue carrier record's ERA write-off rule is set in Master Files, this pass follows every Florida Blue Post.
- **era_clear's Process-Accepted $0 pass (zero_post) moves $0 PR-group denial lines to the patient balance** (PR-22 'other payer primary', PR-177, PR-119 ...): 27 lines zero-posted this run; the PR-22 ones were moved back with `move_to_ins.move` and re-routed Medicare-first (`move_to_ins.move(cid, "<medseq>,<bcbsseq>", rebill_seq=<medseq>)` -> full-visit echo + insorder + freq-1 chargerebill 204; `ins_update.update(pid, medicare_insid, sequence="1,2")` flips the chart default). zero_post also FAULTED once per check on 1052814 and 1052818 (era_clear.log truncates the fault at 200 chars: `"Fault":{"faultcode":"Cl...`) yet still dismissed the checks - the fault text should be logged in full next time.
- **PR-177 (patient not eligible) from Florida Blue on a chart William already ruled on** (pat17477059, 9/14 'write off as uninsured ... no pay bad patient debt'): balance moved back to insurance then `manual_post.post(cid, "BCBS", 0.0, fee, "WOBAD", checknumber="ERA<trace>", ...)` -> success, $0 both sides.
- **Claimocity feed timing:** batch 127783 filled over ~40 minutes (19:14Z 31 visits -> 19:2xZ 60 -> 20:1xZ 101) while the run worked it. A bypass loop that re-lists the batch will see NEW visits that never passed the deceased guard / primary-dx gate: run the guard on every visit the bypass pass touches (guard_sweep_1910.py pattern) and persist the held list (`registers/insp_<stamp>_blocked.json`) so a `--bypass-only` re-run cannot bypass a blocked visit. A no-change full-visit resave (`updvisitwithnewcharges` force=1 inspectorenabled=1) re-runs the inspector and restores the advisory with a fresh error id - the way to put a wrongly bypassed visit back on hold.

- **Deceased guard hits this run** (Stedi Medicare 271 date of death): pat17473477 DOD [DOB], pat45668 DOD [DOB] (both CCM [DOB] visits held; board rows on William's turn).

## 2026-09-22 chat — Medicaid/SMMC Garland-G -> Effinger, full past-year set (William)
- Runner `billing/rebill/medicaid_garland-g_fix.py` (targets from masswriteoffsearch per Medicaid carrier id, DOS >= 1 year): A = prof15 visits -> profile swap to prof13 with refplan=rprov13 main plan in the same resave (AMD voids + recreates on a new visit; recreations start insbilled=0 = already queued); B = prof13 visits on rprov15 charts -> refplan repoint; both freq-1 + chargerebill on the balance-holding plan's sequence. FCC (FLO99) balances route to MCAD FFS when car6113 is on file and no active SMMC plan, to the SMMC plan when one exists, else kept on FLO99 and flagged.
- Lessons: (1) AMD's server clock is ET and can be a calendar day behind the container (UTC) - never match createtime on the container date alone; (2) charge DETAIL reports proccode as pcodeNNNN while gettxhistory reports the CPT - match recreations on FEE + visit DATE, never on proccode, and keep a consumed set so two same-fee visits swapped back-to-back cannot claim one recreation; (3) settling gate matters at scale: 231 of 302 Garland-G-rendered Medicaid visits already carried a posted payment and were NOT re-rendered (that remainder is the WOMCAD/WOCAID2ND write-off case).
- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). Registers: medicaid_garland-g_fix_2026-09-22.txt, verify_recreated_2026-09-22.txt.

The settling gate is what kept those payments from being unapplied.

## 2026-09-22 chat — Medicaid/SMMC Garland-G -> Effinger, full past-year set (William)
- Runner `billing/rebill/medicaid_garland-g_fix.py` (targets from masswriteoffsearch per Medicaid carrier id, DOS >= 1 year): A = prof15 visits -> profile swap to prof13 with refplan=rprov13 main plan in the same resave (AMD voids + recreates on a new visit; recreations start insbilled=0 = already queued); B = prof13 visits on rprov15 charts -> refplan repoint; both freq-1 + chargerebill on the balance-holding plan's sequence. FCC (FLO99) balances route to MCAD FFS when car6113 is on file and no active SMMC plan, to the SMMC plan when one exists, else kept on FLO99 and flagged.
- Lessons: (1) AMD's server clock is ET and can be a calendar day behind the container (UTC) - never match createtime on the container date alone; (2) charge DETAIL reports proccode as pcodeNNNN while gettxhistory reports the CPT - match recreations on FEE + visit DATE, never on proccode, and keep a consumed set so two same-fee visits swapped back-to-back cannot claim one recreation; (3) settling gate matters at scale: 231 of 302 Garland-G-rendered Medicaid visits already carried a posted payment and were NOT re-rendered (that remainder is the WOMCAD/WOCAID2ND write-off case).
- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). Registers: medicaid_garland-g_fix_2026-09-22.txt, verify_recreated_2026-09-22.txt.

## 2026-09-22 chat — Humana + the ~290-visit verification (Garland-G -> Effinger, William confirmed the visits were his)
- Humana (HUM01 car6285, HUMMA1 car302851) through the same runner (now parametrized: TGT / DONEF / LOGF env vars; visit-wide settling gate; B-kind skipped when the visit's plan is already rprov13): 180 visits - 5 re-rendered, 37 repointed, 58 charges rebilled, 138 settling.
- Read-back of all 290 target visits (MCAD, SUN01, WEL04, WEL07, HUM01): 45 re-rendered + rebilled ($6,616), 245 PAID by the payer and left as-is ($8,578 residual = write-off path), 0 still Garland-G-unpaid. CORRECTION to the 9/22 assumption: only WellCare truly rejected Garland-G's NPI (34 of 38 unpaid); Humana paid 138/143, FL Medicaid 69/72, Sunshine 34/37 under her NPI. The settling gate is what kept those payments from being unapplied.
- Past filing limit (DOS > 12 months), Garland-G-rendered, open: $4,722 (FLO99 $1,995 / HUM01 $2,413 / small MCAD, SUN01, WEL04) - not re-rendered; write-off path.

## Additions from the nightly cascade 2026-09-24T0530Z (1:30 AM ET 9/24/2026)
No new call shape. Behaviours learned:
- posterapayments (era_post.post_check) on a check with one bad PaymentEntry line answers the -2147217287 'N payment could not be posted' fault for the WHOLE batch, yet every other line in the batch IS posted (9/24: 113 of 114 lines of check 1052828 landed, unpostedamount fell $11,504.56 -> $278.58 while the reply said Results null / Fault). After such a fault re-read GET /api/era/checks + checkdetails before retrying anything - the remaining lines are the only ones left to post by hand.
- precheck.py CARRIERS: the workspace carriers.json is the rich carN->[code,name] map; the code->carN lookup must go through the manual_post-style normalizer or every non-Medicare pair reads 'no <carrier> plan on chart'.
- gv_rebill.run(pid, seq) sweeps EVERY COB9 $0 posting on the chart for 364 days; when the Medicare record carries a date of death, bound the sweep to DOS <= DOD-1 (hospice_gv_0924_0530.run_bounded) so post-death charges are left to the DOD rule.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-24 0510 UTC (1 AM ET 9/24 slot, ehpa cloud Code Routine, run 2026-09-24T0510Z)

No new call shape this run. Behaviours learned (chart ids only):

- Change Healthcare / Florida Medicaid front-end edit `CH FI PSBR0111: INVALID PAYER RESP SEQ NUM CODE P` on getclaimexclusioncharges = a Florida Medicaid FFS (MCAD car6113) claim sent as PRIMARY for a member Medicaid knows is on an SMMC MMA plan. AMD's free 271 on the Medicaid plan names the plan ('SMMC MMA Capitated FLORIDA COMMUNITY CARE' / 'HUMANA MEDICAL PLAN' / 'SIMPLY HEALTHCARE PLANS'); fix = visit insorder -> that plan's sequence (full-visit updvisitwithcharges echo), freq1_visit.run to it (204), hideexclusions; ins_update.update(pid, insid, sequence="<seq>") sets the PATIENT default order so the next Claimocity batch does not repeat it (10 claims, 5 charts, run 1700236).
- Unbilled 'Insurance order is missing on visit' on an ESTABLISHED chart (September CCM batch 127783, 25 charts) = the PATIENT record's insorder is empty (the seq-1 plan end-dated, or the order never set after a payer switch) so the feed creates the visit with no order. getdemographic <patient insorder=""> shows it. Fix per visit = full-visit resave with insorder + onhcfa=1 and a freq-1 original; the durable fix = ins_update sequence= on the patient (26 charts this run). 'Date of service is outside insurance coverage effective dates' = the visit insorder still lists an end-dated plan (e.g. '2,1' with seq 1 ended) - drop it from the visit order. 'When billing Medicare as the non-primary carrier, the MSP Code ... is required' = Medicare sits behind a Medicare Advantage plan in the order - the MA plan stands alone (Medicare never secondary to its own MA plan).
- getclaiminspectorcharges: the error element's `name` attribute is a truncated label ('The primary procedure code on Claim ID...'); the pattern text ('Pattern 35790 ...') is the element BODY (e.text) - match advisories on the body, not the name. Every resave re-inspects and re-issues ids (bypass only from a fresh listing).
- Stedi UHC 87726 eligibility with the MBI as memberId returns the UHC Medicare Advantage member number (subscriber.memberId) and plan name - the way to get the MA id when the MBI lookup names a UHC entity (Care Improvement Plus = UHC) but no number. CHP 95112 with the MBI answers AAA 72 (CHP needs its own id); insurance discovery on a 45-year-old disabled beneficiary returned CMS + FL Medicaid QI1 but not the CHP MA member id.
- Stedi MBILUNOSSN by name/DOB answered ERR 72 for four HCA admits who ARE Medicare Advantage members (discovery then named the UHC MA plan and member id) - ERR 72 on the MBI lookup does not rule out an MA member; run discovery before calling a chart 'not Medicare'.
- addr_line1_fix.fix on the Claimocity HCA charts: line 1 filled (200) after the email-field phone is blanked (500 'Email is not a valid format' on the first try); the clear-line-2 PATCH still answers 500 (cosmetic, line 2 repeats the street).
- ScanSnap PDFs are not always remits: the 9/24 03:39Z pair in 'bht Optum era downloads' is the Optum/TRICARE East ERA enrollment form + PGBA EDI trading partner agreement (image-only; pdftoppm -r 110 + tesseract reads them) - correspondence -> board row, nothing posted.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-24 0710 UTC (3 AM ET 9/24 slot, ehpa cloud Code Routine, run 2026-09-24T0710Z)

No new call shape this run. Behaviours (chart ids only):

- `POST /api/era/locks/release-expired` with a JSON object body (`{}`) answers HTTP 400 "Unexpected character encountered while parsing value: {" - the shape era_post.locks() sends is the accepted one; call the helper, not amd.rest_post with a dict.
- `GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid=` on a check the Post button has already worked returns ONLY the claims still unposted (eracheckdetails had 1 claim for 1052828 after 114 of 115 visits posted); the posted claims drop off the detail, so a cache taken before posting is the only record of the whole check.
- era_clear.py --dry on a check whose only open money is a residual on an already-adjudicated charge (ERA paid 68.62, live insurance balance 0.00, the payment on the charge carries a different check number) reports it as 'postable money' and HOLDS the check - the residual rule (William 9/13) is applied by the run report, not by era_clear; the check stays on the unposted list.
- Columns unchanged for a whole slot (0/20/25/2, all standing holds, no new claim run): the run's only AMD writes were era_post.locks; nothing else touched.

# captured_calls additions - run 2026-09-24T1310Z (9am ET 9/24/2026, ehpa cloud Code Routine)
No new call shape this run. Behaviours confirmed:
- getclaimexclusioncharges (clearinghouse=1 payer=1) on run 1700228 returned two rows whose <error> carries isexclusion="0" and name 'PA  [name] AS UNPRCESSBLE CLM- [name] BEEN REJECTD AND NOT ENTERD INTO ADJUDICATION SYS.:DUPLICATE OF A PREVIOUSLY PROCESSED CLAIM, LINE.' (+ the long-form 'PA R ACKNOWLEDGEMENT/RETURNED AS UNPROCESSABLE CLAIM ...') = Humana payer-level duplicate rejection (payer id 2449). hideexclusions claimrunid=1700228 hide=-1 with <visit id claimid/> x2 -> success=1; the run re-lists empty. Both visits had been re-sent by a 9/22 referral-plan repoint resave (billdate 09/22/2026) on claims Humana had already adjudicated (pat17472396 paid, pat44953 F0/107 $0) - a resave that re-queues a claim to a payer that already adjudicated it draws this rejection; completing it is the whole fix.
- gettxhistory charge attrs on the Humana-paid CCM (pat17472396 v19448871): carrier=HUM01, paid=189.86/100.08, insbal=16.14/8.92 (coinsurance residual), payerinfo 'Payor ID: 2449'.
- ERA chain on check 1052828 unchanged from 0710Z/0910Z/1113Z: era_post --all-unposted -> fault -2147217287 (Error Counters PaymentEntry=1) on the over-apply guard visit; era_fallback_0913b classifies residual-zero $68.62 / already $37.13; era_clear holds ('postable money' 1). GET /api/era/checks before = after (1 / $105.75). POST /api/era/locks/release-expired sent.
- Bootstrap in a Claude Code cloud session: the repo clone lives at /home/user/effinger-billing-backend on a session branch; `git worktree add -B main /home/claude/effinger-billing-backend origin/main` gives the routine its /home/claude/effinger-billing-backend main checkout without touching the session branch.

# captured_calls additions - run 2026-09-25T0510Z (1am ET 9/25/2026, ehpa cloud Code Routine)
No new call shape this run. Behaviours confirmed:
- Unbilled 'Payment reason codes are missing or do not balance' on a visit whose ERA line was MANUAL-posted (addpayments, no payreason children): payreasons.py --live --unbilled voids the code-less payment + its same-second WOINS, reposts the line through posterapayments (ERA-linked, CO45/PR2/CO253 reasons from the era_details cache) and releases the visit - and the ERA check itself (1052828, on the unposted list since 9/24 with PaymentEntry=1 refusals) LEFT GET /api/era/checks on that repost: the ERA-linked repost of the last open money line is what clears a check the Post button and era_clear both refused. Lesson: on a check the Post button refuses because one line was already partly adjudicated, post the open line ERA-linked (payreasons.repost_visit shape) rather than manual_post - the manual post draws the Unbilled reason-code error and leaves the check on the list.
- getclaimunbillablecharges after the nightly claim run (1700236 still newest at 05:18Z): 40 rows vs 25 at 03:19Z - the 9/24 evening claim run refreshed the snapshot and re-listed 13 'prior payor' visits (charge carrier FLO02, billdate 9/5-9/13, $0 paid, a $0 BCBS/UHC denial on each) plus two 9/22 SNF visits from the Claimocity feed. One row (pat23391 v19392347) appears TWICE in the XML (a header block with no error and the BCBS block with two errors) - dedupe on visit id before releasing.
- Claimocity feed duplicate chart: lookuppatient name="OSGOOD,[first]%" returned two charts with the same DOB (pat17470849 MARIAN, pat17477870 RAVENSCAR, chart numbers 1946 / 4826). addinsurance on the new chart worked (three plans, sequences 1-3); checkEligibility on the copied UHC plan answered AAA 73 (subscriber name) - the payer keys on the spelling, so a duplicate chart cannot bill a UHC MA plan until it is merged or renamed. family_address on a chart with NO responsible-party address answers 500 'Address is required.' (same as 9/12) - only the UI Edit address creates the first address.
- amdw.resave_visit(pid, [both charge ids], "2") + ins_update.update(pid, insid, sequence="2") + addr_line1_fix.fix(pid, "ALF", ...) + hideunbillablevisits = the whole 'seq-1 plan end-dated, Medicare seq 2' release in four calls (Hollis pattern = Brinson 9/13); readback insorder via amdw.detail(cid)[0]["insorder"].

## 2026-09-22 chat — Medicaid/SMMC Garland-G -> Effinger, full past-year set (William)
- Runner `billing/rebill/medicaid_garland-g_fix.py` (targets from masswriteoffsearch per Medicaid carrier id, DOS >= 1 year): A = prof15 visits -> profile swap to prof13 with refplan=rprov13 main plan in the same resave (AMD voids + recreates on a new visit; recreations start insbilled=0 = already queued); B = prof13 visits on rprov15 charts -> refplan repoint; both freq-1 + chargerebill on the balance-holding plan's sequence. FCC (FLO99) balances route to MCAD FFS when car6113 is on file and no active SMMC plan, to the SMMC plan when one exists, else kept on FLO99 and flagged.
- Lessons: (1) AMD's server clock is ET and can be a calendar day behind the container (UTC) - never match createtime on the container date alone; (2) charge DETAIL reports proccode as pcodeNNNN while gettxhistory reports the CPT - match recreations on FEE + visit DATE, never on proccode, and keep a consumed set so two same-fee visits swapped back-to-back cannot claim one recreation; (3) settling gate matters at scale: 231 of 302 Garland-G-rendered Medicaid visits already carried a posted payment and were NOT re-rendered (that remainder is the WOMCAD/WOCAID2ND write-off case).
- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). Registers: medicaid_garland-g_fix_2026-09-22.txt, verify_recreated_2026-09-22.txt.

# captured_calls additions - run 2026-09-25T0910Z (5am ET 9/25/2026, ehpa cloud Code Routine)
No new call shape this run. Behaviours confirmed:
- A Medicare ERA of 93 paid claims (check 1052840) posted through posterapayments in ONE call (era_post --all-unposted, 93 visits, HTTP 200, PPMDResults Error null); the live charges then read payment + WOINS contractual per line with the PR coinsurance left as insurance balance for the secondary (BCBS / AAR01 / MCAD in insorder) or moved to the patient balance where the chart has no secondary - the Medicare carrier's ERA setup writes off CO-45 by itself (unlike Florida Blue / VA CCN).
- A $0 Medicare ERA of denial lines only (check 1052841: OA-18, CO-29, CO-16) is held by era_clear as 'denial with write-off (cascade handles)' and dismissed (POST /api/era/checks/dismiss) because no postable money remains; its lines are classified from era_details (carc/rarc per charge) plus gettxhistory per chart.
- OA-18 (MA01) from Medicare on a visit whose charges already carry a non-void FLO02 payment = a frequency-1 resend of an adjudicated claim; the original adjudication is the check number on that payment - recorded 'duplicate-original-<check>' in registers/era_manual_register.json, nothing posted, never rebilled. gettxhistory payment elements carry checknumber/date/amount/carrier but NOT origrefnum (the ICN is on the getchargedetaildataicd10 payment element).
- Stedi insurance-discovery answered HTTP 502 once and succeeded on the retry 20 s later; MBI lookup ERR 72 + AMD 271 AAA 72 on the same chart while a Medicare payment sits on it from 5/28/2026 = the MBI has most likely been reissued (board row for the card).
- manual_post.post(cid, 'FLO02', 0.0, <insbalance>, 'WOLATE', checknumber='<ERA TRN>', depositdate='<check date>', note=...) posts a bare write-off against an ERA denial line (addpayments, live balance re-read first, readback insbalance 0.00).

# captured_calls additions - run 2026-09-25T1310Z (9am ET 9/25/2026, ehpa cloud Code Routine)
No new call shape this run. Behaviours confirmed:
- getclaimcenteroverview at 13:20Z: chargereview 0 / inspector 20 ($3,846) / unbilled 26 ($5,252) / exclusions 8 ($1,268 - the overview count lags; getclaimexclusioncharges per run lists 2) / claimrunstatus 83 runs; newest claim run still 1700236 - the four list calls returned exactly the 1110Z rows (same 20 inspector error ids).
- GET /api/era/checks listed ONE new check (1052842 WEL04, trace 1004607063, $226.97, 2 claims); GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid=1052842 -> 2 claims, each 1 charge, carc CO45 + PI253 (sequestration) with payreasonwoamount 0.0 / wocode blank; posterapayments (era_post --all-unposted) -> HTTP 200, 2 visits posted; the check left the unposted list on its own (after snapshot empty).
- POST-BUTTON BEHAVIOUR (WellCare, new evidence 9/25): gettxhistory read back after the post shows the payment with <payreason> CO45 + PI253 on it (checknumber = the TRN) but insbal = fee - paid (153.49 / 40.54), patbal 0 - i.e. AMD posted the reason codes and did NOT adjust the contractual; because the chart carries a seq-2 plan (GEO05 car6128) the residual stayed on the INSURANCE balance instead of the patient (the Florida Blue / VA CCN cases of 9/23 landed on the patient). Correction = manual_post.post(chargeid, 'WEL04', 0.0, <CO45+PI253>, 'WOINS', checknumber=<TRN>, depositdate=<check date>) -> addpayments success (paymentid 10164236 / 10164237), insbal 0.00 on readback. Rule stands: after every Post button run, read the posted charges back and write off the CO/PI contractual the poster left open.
- payreasons.py --list on an Unbilled list with no 'Payment reason codes' rows prints 0 rows / SUMMARY {} and makes no write.

## 2026-09-22 chat — Medicaid/SMMC Garland-G -> Effinger, full past-year set (William)
- Runner `billing/rebill/medicaid_garland-g_fix.py` (targets from masswriteoffsearch per Medicaid carrier id, DOS >= 1 year): A = prof15 visits -> profile swap to prof13 with refplan=rprov13 main plan in the same resave (AMD voids + recreates on a new visit; recreations start insbilled=0 = already queued); B = prof13 visits on rprov15 charts -> refplan repoint; both freq-1 + chargerebill on the balance-holding plan's sequence. FCC (FLO99) balances route to MCAD FFS when car6113 is on file and no active SMMC plan, to the SMMC plan when one exists, else kept on FLO99 and flagged.
- Lessons: (1) AMD's server clock is ET and can be a calendar day behind the container (UTC) - never match createtime on the container date alone; (2) charge DETAIL reports proccode as pcodeNNNN while gettxhistory reports the CPT - match recreations on FEE + visit DATE, never on proccode, and keep a consumed set so two same-fee visits swapped back-to-back cannot claim one recreation; (3) settling gate matters at scale: 231 of 302 Garland-G-rendered Medicaid visits already carried a posted payment and were NOT re-rendered (that remainder is the WOMCAD/WOCAID2ND write-off case).
- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). - Unbilled 'A payment from the prior payor is required' rows appear when a payer posts a $0 PR-27 onto a charge whose LIVE carrier is already the re-routed primary (UHC check W0384723 9/25 on pat24853's three 99233s billed to Humana 9/23): the charge shows carrier HUM01 / billdate 9/23 with the UNI20 $0 payment attached; patient balance stays 0; hideunbillablevisits hide=-1 (numeric visit id) -> success=1, and the after-list dropped by all five released visits (the Delmar visit had TWO rows - blank carrier and BCBS - both left with one release).
- ins_update.update(pid, insid, sequence="2,3") answers <Results success="1"><patient insorder="2,3"/> and getdemographic reads the new patient insorder at once (pat17473343: '3' -> '2,3').
- payreasons.dismissed_index() lists 1750 dismissed checks since 8/1; payreasons.details(<dismissed checkid>) returns the eracheckdetails envelope with NO claim lines for a dismissed check (the 9/19 restore recipe is still the only way to read a dismissed check's lines).

## 2026-09-22 chat — Medicaid/SMMC Garland-G -> Effinger, full past-year set (William)
- Runner `billing/rebill/medicaid_garland-g_fix.py` (targets from masswriteoffsearch per Medicaid carrier id, DOS >= 1 year): A = prof15 visits -> profile swap to prof13 with refplan=rprov13 main plan in the same resave (AMD voids + recreates on a new visit; recreations start insbilled=0 = already queued); B = prof13 visits on rprov15 charts -> refplan repoint; both freq-1 + chargerebill on the balance-holding plan's sequence. FCC (FLO99) balances route to MCAD FFS when car6113 is on file and no active SMMC plan, to the SMMC plan when one exists, else kept on FLO99 and flagged.
- Lessons: (1) AMD's server clock is ET and can be a calendar day behind the container (UTC) - never match createtime on the container date alone; (2) charge DETAIL reports proccode as pcodeNNNN while gettxhistory reports the CPT - match recreations on FEE + visit DATE, never on proccode, and keep a consumed set so two same-fee visits swapped back-to-back cannot claim one recreation; (3) settling gate matters at scale: 231 of 302 Garland-G-rendered Medicaid visits already carried a posted payment and were NOT re-rendered (that remainder is the WOMCAD/WOCAID2ND write-off case).
- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). Registers: medicaid_garland-g_fix_2026-09-22.txt, verify_recreated_2026-09-22.txt.

## 2026-09-22 chat — Humana + the ~290-visit verification (Garland-G -> Effinger, William confirmed the visits were his)
- Humana (HUM01 car6285, HUMMA1 car302851) through the same runner (now parametrized: TGT / DONEF / LOGF env vars; visit-wide settling gate; B-kind skipped when the visit's plan is already rprov13): 180 visits - 5 re-rendered, 37 repointed, 58 charges rebilled, 138 settling.
- Read-back of all 290 target visits (MCAD, SUN01, WEL04, WEL07, HUM01): 45 re-rendered + rebilled ($6,616), 245 PAID by the payer and left as-is ($8,578 residual = write-off path), 0 still Garland-G-unpaid. CORRECTION to the 9/22 assumption: only WellCare truly rejected Garland-G's NPI (34 of 38 unpaid); Humana paid 138/143, FL Medicaid 69/72, Sunshine 34/37 under her NPI. The settling gate is what kept those payments from being unapplied.
- Past filing limit (DOS > 12 months), Garland-G-rendered, open: $4,722 (FLO99 $1,995 / HUM01 $2,413 / small MCAD, SUN01, WEL04) - not re-rendered; write-off path.

# captured_calls additions - run 2026-09-28T2310Z (7pm ET 9/28/2026, ehpa cloud Code Routine)
No new call shape this run. Behaviours confirmed / learned:
- Credential: the ehpa cloud environment now carries the POI0712 password as AMD_PSW (env var) - .amd_cred.json written from it, no Gmail read; ONE amd.login_cached success. The session's auto-mode classifier refused ONE Bash call this run ('Modify Shared Resources': a script adding a secondary plan on pat20729 from Pam's sheet, chained with precheck.py) - both left undone per the no-workaround rule; every other AMD write (addinsurance x10, updextrainsinfo x~70, updvisitwithcharges/updvisitwithnewcharges x~75, hideunbillablevisits, hideexclusions, chargerebill x8, addpayments via payreasons.post_line) ran normally.
- Stedi Humana 61101 eligibility with the MBI as member id returns the Humana MA member number (H-number) and plan name for a brand-new Claimocity chart - the UHC-87726 pattern works for Humana too (2 charts this run: HumanaChoice Florida PPO, 'HUM Full Ac Giveback' / Emphesys). CHP 95112 with the MBI answers AAA 72 (CHP wants its own id - 2 charts held). Stedi insurance discovery answered HTTP 502 Bad Gateway twice (transient - a retry answered); discovery with the chart's out-of-state address (GA) found the Medicare + Humana Honor coverage that the FL lookups missed.
- A CMS 271 'other payer' entry of type OT (code R) naming UNITEDHEALTHCARE INS. CO. & UHC INS. CO. OF NY with no HN/PR entry = an AARP Medigap; UHC 87726 echoes its member id with an empty planStatus. AAR01 plan added with that id: AMD's 271 on it answered ACTIVE (plan 20260101) on one chart and REJECTED 72 on the other.
- getclaimexclusioncharges after hideexclusions on run 1700243 (6 duplicates) returned an EMPTY visit list although the two pat21489 rows were still open - the re-list right after a hide can lag; the after-scan 30 min later listed them until they were completed.

- getclaimcenteroverview at 23:18Z: chargereview 0 / inspector 90 ($16,747) / unbilled 56 ($11,040.97) / exclusions 19 ($3,892 - the per-run list calls returned 9) / claimrunstatus 86; at 00:0xZ after: inspector 25 ($4,595) / unbilled 38 ($7,450.97).

## 2026-09-29 0110 UTC - 9 PM ET 9/28 backend billing run (ehpa cloud) - behaviours learned, no new call shape
- PCC 'Admission Record Report' PDFs that William prints for a WHOLE facility (Brynwood 39 residents, Aviata 37 residents; 700-950 KB) come through the Drive connector's read_file_content as 170-190 KB of text (the tool saves it to a file when it exceeds the reply cap) - split on 'ADMISSION RECORD' per resident; the payer block reads 'Primary Payer <plan> ... Second Payer Medicare A coinsurance from Medicaid Medicaid # <id> Ins. Company <SMMC plan>' (Brynwood layout) or 'HMO/Managed Care Name: <plan> ... HMO/Managed Policy#: <id> ... Primary Payer MCDP-Medicaid Pending Florida' (Aviata layout). Mask SSNs before saving. A census settles a secondary without a lookup and catches JPEG mis-reads (pat20729: photo read <photo-read id ...504647> -> census <census Medicaid id ...004647>).
- Stedi Centene 68069 on a Sunshine LTC-dual member answers planDetails 'Medicaid LTC Dual' + 'SSHP - LTC without HCBS Dual Eligible' with planBegin (20260901 here) and no errors; a one-digit-off id answers ERR 72 - the cheap way to choose between two candidate Medicaid numbers before add_ins.
- amd.check_eligibility(insplan_id) returns a TUPLE in this amd.py build (not the raw XML string) - take [1] before regex-parsing; elig_amd.py <pid> --all is the safer wrapper (summary lines per plan, saved under patient_db/<pid>/).
- A full-visit resave with approval="1" inspectorenabled="1" (used here only to set insorder 1,2 on 7 unpaid visits) re-inspects the visit and re-raises Pattern 11256 that an earlier run bypassed - always re-list the batches and bypass again with the fresh error ids after any resave (columns_inspector2 rule confirmed).
- Unbilled 'A payment from the prior payor is required' rows released on 9/26 (rule b) reappear after the next payment event on the chart; releasing again is correct as long as the charge's carrier/billdate show the claim is still out and unanswered (7 rows this run).

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-29 1110 UTC (7 AM ET) ehpa cloud run

No new call shapes this run. Lessons recorded against existing calls:

- **posterapayments (the ERA Center Post button) leaves CO-45 on the balance for FLORIDA MEDICARE too** (9/29/2026, ERA 1052872, ACH 819872046, 32 claims / 39 lines): every line carried the CO45 + CO253 reasons on the payment with `payreasonwoamount 0`, so after the post each charge showed insurance balance = CO + PR (patient balance on the 3 charts with no secondary). Correction = the 9/23 pass: `move_to_ins.move(cid)` where the balance sits on the patient, then `manual_post.post(cid, "FLO02", 0.0, CO45+CO253, "WOINS", checknumber=<trace>, depositdate=<check date>)`; PR-2 is left for the crossover. Script `era_co45_fix_1110.py` (reads the check details, refuses any line whose live balance != CO + PR). Carriers now known to need the pass: Florida Blue, VA CCN, CAP07/UHC, Ambetter, Humana, Medicaid FFS, Florida Medicare.
- **A $0 CO-29 (N211) line on a Medicare remit can be a frequency-1 resubmission of a claim Medicare already PAID** - read the live charge before treating it as untimely: pat17470697 x9, pat17468923, pat17470939 (ERA 1052873) all carried a 9/9/2026 Medicare payment; the 9/22 resend drew CO-29. No WOLATE; what was missing was the original remit's contractual. When the original remit is not in the cache, Medicare Part B numbers reconstruct it exactly: allowed = paid / 0.784 (80% less the 2% sequestration), PR-2 = 20% of allowed, WOINS = charge - allowed + CO-253 (= live insurance balance - PR-2); verified against sibling lines that carried the 9/9 WOINS (33.76 on 99232 / 43.80 on 99233).
- **CO-109 + N418 on a Medicare remit = an MA enrollment the chart does not show**: AMD's free 271 on the Medicare plan lists the MA contract under the other-payer EB (e.g. `PR 18:H9572 BCBS OF MICHIGAN`), Stedi's MBI lookup at the DOS confirms the COB date, and the MA payer's own 271 (Stedi `BCBSF` / `87726` with the chart member id at that DOS) closes the cascade - then plan begin/end + patient insorder + visit insorder + freq-1.
- Stedi claim status v2 (`claim_status_sweep.status(payer, first, last, dob, member, dos, amount)` returns `(http, json)`) answered Humana 61101 with P0/45 'pending, awaiting benefit determination' + the payer claim number for both duplicate-rejected CCM claims - the fastest way to prove a PA 'duplicate of a previously processed claim' rejection needs no rebill.

## 2026-09-29 15:10 UTC (11 AM ET 9/29/2026) - ehpa cloud backend run 2026-09-29T1510Z
No new call shapes this run (vocabulary unchanged); every write used registered calls (posterapayments via era_post --all-unposted, era_clear dismiss, move_to_ins updvisitwithcharges, bypassclaimedits, checkEligibility, Stedi MBI lookup / discovery / eligibility). Lessons:
- **CHP ERA 4124256 (ERA 1052877) posted CLEAN through the Post button**: all 23 money lines (CO-45 + CO-253, PR 0) landed with the contractual written off - every charge $0 both sides on the live read, so the 9/23-9/25 'contractual on the patient balance' correction pass was not needed on this CHP check. Keep reading live after every Post button; the defect is not constant.
- **CHP PR-200 'expenses incurred during lapse in coverage' ($0 claims)**: era_clear's zero-post pushes the whole charge to the PATIENT balance (same as the UHC PR-27 lesson of 9/25) - move_to_ins.move(cid) each charge back before the cascade. The cascade on both charts (pat21483, pat22322): CHP 271 AAA 75 (subscriber not found), Stedi MBILUNOSSN = straight Medicare ACTIVE (no Advantage entity), discovery adds only PDP / 'contact entity' rows. A move-back resave of a CCM visit (99487+99489) re-inspects it and raises Pattern 35790 - bypass with the fresh error id.
- **Environment guard**: the auto-mode classifier declined the payer-switch write (plan add + end date + order + re-send) as a real-world transaction this slot; the single-purpose writes (Post button, dismiss, move_to_ins, bypass) were allowed. The trail is on the board rows for William's decision.

- **ERA carrier mapping**: AMD's ERA carrier table maps ERA carrier id 8129 "AARP SUPPLEMENTAL HEALTH PLANS FROM UNITEDHEALTHCARE" to systemcarrier FLO99 (FLORIDA COMMUNITY CARE AVAILITY) - check 1052887 posted AARP Medigap money under FLO99 on pat17477259. ERA carrier id 3429 (same payer name) maps to UNI20. The checkdetails payload carries no member id, so a missing Medigap plan cannot be added from the 835. Master Files item for William.
