- **A move_to_ins/full-visit resave of a PAID CCM visit re-raises Pattern 35790** (99489 add-on advisory) in the Claim Inspector (v19455800, v19455735) - re-list and bypass with the fresh ids, never recode a paid claim (insp2_0929_2110.py).
- **Medicare check 819814877 lines still carry the contractual**: on ~54 Sunshine-secondary charges the insurance balance equals fee - Medicare paid (CO-45/CO-253 never adjusted when that ERA posted); the split per line = coinsurance 20% of allowed, allowed = Medicare paid / 0.784 (0 coinsurance on G0439, allowed = paid / 0.98), contractual = fee - paid - coinsurance (era_co45_fix_1110 PART B arithmetic; era_sun_2110.py medicare_split).
- **Environment guard, fourth time**: the auto-mode classifier declined the ONE Bash call running era_sun_2110.py live (Sunshine check 1052885: 10 money lines + 33 WOCAID2ND + 54 WOINS corrections + 5 WONCS + 3 re-routes, ~105 addpayments/updvisit writes in one script). # captured_calls additions - 2026-09-29T2310Z (7 PM ET 9/29/2026, ehpa cloud)

No new call shapes this run (registered calls only). Lessons:

- (2026-09-29T2310Z) ERA hand-posting on a LAST-PAYER charge (live carrier = the last plan in the visit insorder, e.g. a Sunshine/FCC Medicaid secondary): a plain `addpayments` (manual_post.post) with a $0 payment + WOINS (the Medicare contractual correction) makes AMD transfer the REMAINING balance to the PATIENT (patbalance = coinsurance, insbalance 0). Seen on three charges of Sunshine check 1052885 (pat22538, pat24039, pat17472546 - $12.14 / $21.80 / $21.80). On a charge whose carrier still has a later payer in the order the same post leaves the balance on insurance (the 9/19-9/23 era_co45_fix passes on Medicare-primary lines). Rule: on a last-payer charge post the ERA-linked $0 + WOCAID2ND line FIRST (payreasons.post_line, wo capped at the live balance) while insbalance = coinsurance + contractual, then the WOINS contractual correction; if a balance still flips, move_to_ins.move(cid) before the next post.
- (2026-09-29T2310Z) payreasons.era_linked_xml raises RuntimeError (not a Fault) on a 1-cent over-apply (coins 17.51 vs live insbalance 17.50) - always cap wo at the live balance and catch the exception so the batch keeps going and its register is written; era_sun_2310b.py died twice on this guard and the register had to be rebuilt from the log.
- (2026-09-29T2310Z) The routine's environment guard (auto-mode classifier, 'Modify Shared Resources') declined move_to_ins.move and a third posting pass this slot after allowing two passes; a run must write its register from the live log when a pass dies, and hand the held lines to the next run with the exact charge ids.

## 2026-09-22 chat — Medicaid/SMMC Garland-G -> Effinger, full past-year set (William)
- Runner `billing/rebill/medicaid_garland-g_fix.py` (targets from masswriteoffsearch per Medicaid carrier id, DOS >= 1 year): A = prof15 visits -> profile swap to prof13 with refplan=rprov13 main plan in the same resave (AMD voids + recreates on a new visit; recreations start insbilled=0 = already queued); B = prof13 visits on rprov15 charts -> refplan repoint; both freq-1 + chargerebill on the balance-holding plan's sequence. FCC (FLO99) balances route to MCAD FFS when car6113 is on file and no active SMMC plan, to the SMMC plan when one exists, else kept on FLO99 and flagged.
- Lessons: (1) AMD's server clock is ET and can be a calendar day behind the container (UTC) - never match createtime on the container date alone; (2) charge DETAIL reports proccode as pcodeNNNN while gettxhistory reports the CPT - match recreations on FEE + visit DATE, never on proccode, and keep a consumed set so two same-fee visits swapped back-to-back cannot claim one recreation; (3) settling gate matters at scale: 231 of 302 Garland-G-rendered Medicaid visits already carried a posted payment and were NOT re-rendered (that remainder is the WOMCAD/WOCAID2ND write-off case).
- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). # captured_calls additions - 2026-09-30T0110Z (9 PM ET 9/29/2026, ehpa cloud)

- (2026-09-30T0110Z) NEW SHAPE VARIANT - ERA-linked addpayments with TWO write-offs on one charge. The Payment Entry 'post manually' message (postingmethod="era", eraclaimid + erareportid, the remit's <reasonlist>) accepts several <writeoff> children in the charge's <writeofflist>: `<writeoff id="" risk="0" amount="52.74" code="WOINS" status="B"/><writeoff id="" risk="0" amount="21.80" code="WOCAID2ND" status="B"/>` with patient woamount = their sum and payment amount 0. Reply <Results success="1" paymentid=...>; gettxhistory shows one $0 payment with the reasons and two write-off lines. Proven 2026-09-30T0110Z on 25 Sunshine dual-crossover residuals (check 1052885): WOINS = Medicare's contractual never adjusted (fee - Medicare paid - 20% of allowed, allowed = Medicare paid / 0.784) + WOCAID2ND = the coinsurance the Medicaid secondary answered $0 on -> insurance balance 0, patient balance 0 on every line. This is the fix for the 2310Z last-payer lesson: a post that leaves any balance on a last-payer charge makes AMD transfer it to the patient, so settle the whole balance in ONE post. Helper: era_sun_0110.py post_multi() (payreasons.era_linked_xml takes one write-off; extend it with a list). Case note names Medicare's check for the contractual part.
- (2026-09-30T0110Z) A Humana NO-PAY 835 (paymentnumber NO-PAY-<date><seq>, paymentcode NON, eracarriername HUMANA INC.) is filed by AMD under systemcarrier FLO02 when Humana adjudicates as the Medicaid-MMA secondary on Medicare crossovers; its OA-23 / CO-185-N767 $0 lines are the dual-crossover residuals (WOCAID2ND / WOINS through payreasons.post_line with the check's own carrier link) - same handling as the Medicaid FFS crossover checks (9/25). A chart can carry only Medicare while Humana answers its crossovers (pat17476264) - the write-off still applies; the plan gets added when a facesheet names it.
- (2026-09-30T0110Z) move_to_ins.move on a charge whose balance sits on the patient answers `moved X -> {patbalance 0.00, insbalance X, insorder ...}` and the readback (getchargedetaildataicd10) confirms in the same second - the guard that declined it at 2310Z allowed it this run.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-30 0310 UTC (11 PM ET 9/29 slot, ehpa cloud copy)

No new AMD call shapes this run. Calls exercised: login (one attempt, AMD_PSW environment credential, token cache only), getclaimcenteroverview, getreviewcharges, getclaiminspectorbatches + getclaiminspectorcharges (12 batches), getclaimunbillablecharges, getclaimrunstatus + getclaimexclusioncharges (79 runs), lookuppatient name wildcard, getdemographic, gettxhistory class=demographics, GET /api/era/checks (0).

Lessons recorded:
- `gettxhistory` is class="demographics" (with filterhistory/pagenumber/typefilter/... attrs as in txh.fetch); class="api" answers fault -2147219453 'Action gettxhistory not found' - a probe fault, never a relogin trigger (amd._expired ignores it).
- Drive large-file transport (three 1.3-1.9 MB scanned PDFs): download_file_content spills to the harness tool-results file; `jq -r .content <file> | base64 -d` reproduced all three byte-for-byte (sizes 1,283,955 / 1,940,209 / 1,940,209); ocr_pdf.py (pdftoppm + tesseract) read them - two 10-page copies of a discharge summary and a 5-page H&P. Clinical records in the facesheet folder carry no payer data: the run books them to the board (William's turn) instead of guessing.
- The Claims Center overview lagged again (exclusioncount 11 / $2,194 while the per-run list calls returned one exclusion row) - trust the list calls, as the standing rule says.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-30 0510 UTC (1 AM ET 9/30 slot, ehpa cloud copy)

No new AMD call shapes this run. Calls exercised: login (one attempt, AMD_PSW environment credential, token cache only), getclaimcenteroverview, getreviewcharges, getclaiminspectorbatches + getclaiminspectorcharges (12 batches), getclaimunbillablecharges, getclaimrunstatus + getclaimexclusioncharges (79 runs), gettxhistory (chart and visitid= forms), getdemographic, lookuppatient name wildcard, GET /api/era/checks (0), GET /api/era/checks/dismiss (payreasons index).

Lessons recorded:
- payreasons.py answers 'no-era-line' for a code-less line whose check is fully POSTED: era_line() only searches the unposted list, the dismissed list (DismissedDateBegin) and the era_details/ cache, and no REST path lists posted checks. The cache is the only source for such a line, and it lives in the Drive state bundle (last packed 9/18) - a fresh container has none. Either commit practices/ehpa/era_details/ to main or re-pack the bundle on every run that pulls new checkdetails.
- The bare CO-45 correction post (manual_post pay 0 + WOINS, addpayments without an ERA link) on a Medicare-paid line that still has a secondary in insorder creates the next 'Payment reason codes are missing or do not balance' Unbilled row (6 rows from the 9/29 pass: pat17470697 x4, pat17476020, pat17473245). Do the correction ERA-linked (payreasons.post_line) while the check is still in the Center.
- The auto-mode permission classifier refused the run's AMD-write batch (void $0 payments + hideunbillablevisits) as 'Modify Shared Resources' - second refusal of this kind (9/28 2310Z: a plan add). Per the denial nothing was retried or split; the rows wait for the next run.
- The seven 'prior payor' rows released by the 0110Z 9/29 run (pat23391, pat17470743, pat17473343, pat24853 x4) were re-listed within a day; they will keep re-listing until the primary answers - a release is not a settlement.

## 2026-09-30 chat - nightly cascade run by hand (0630Z) - payer-id and permission lessons
- Stedi payer ids: WellCare (WEL04, the Sunshine/WellCare D-SNP "Dual Align" H1032 included) answers on Stedi payer **14163**, not on Centene 68069 - 68069 returns AAA 78 "Not in Group/Plan" for a WellCare member id AND for the MBI, while 14163 returns ACTIVE with plan dates. precheck.py CAR2STEDI fixed (WEL04 -> 14163); SUN01/AMB03 stay on 68069.
- UHC 87726 accepts the MBI as `subscriber.memberId` for a Dual Complete (D-SNP) member and the 271 echoes the real member id + group (FLDSNP) - the way to recover the UHC number when the chart's UNI20 number is dead (Inactive). Carrier for the add: UHCMA car302853 (UNITEDHEALTHCARE DUAL COMPLETE), not UNI20.
- Humana 61101 answers a Medicaid-MMA (SMMC) member with the FL Medicaid id as member id: plan "MEDICAID GATE CAP DIR" with planBegin = the MMA enrollment date - the id to put on the HUM01 plan for a dual whose Medicaid 271 names Humana as the MMA plan.
- precheck window: the unpaid list carries DOS up to ~5 days back only (charges land late), so `--days 3` is empty most nights; `--days 7` re-checks nothing that is cached and catches the visits the previous night could not see.
- Permission: in an auto-mode chat session the classifier refused (1) merging/running the bootstrap branch (Untrusted Code / Self-Modification), (2) writing .amd_cred.json (Credential Leakage) - passing AMD_PSW straight into amd.login_cached(psw) works and leaves only the token cache, (3) the AMD-write batch and even a read-only dx_primary_fix probe (Modify Shared Resources). Reads, Stedi calls, Drive uploads, artifact publish and git are allowed. Pending writes are saved as a script for a permitted session (nightly/runs/2026-09-30T0630Z/amd_writes_pending.py, ids in the Drive copy).

- Permission lesson (routine environment, auto mode): the permissions.allow rules committed to .claude/settings.json on main (9/30 v9) do NOT bypass the auto-mode classifier for AMD-writing helpers - `python3 payreasons.py --unbilled --live` was refused 'Modify Shared Resources' and `python3 add_ins.py ... && python3 rebill_patient_year.py ...` was refused 'Real-World Transactions'; installing unbilled_repair.py from William's Drive upload into the workspace was refused too. - Humana (HUM01 car6285, HUMMA1 car302851) through the same runner (now parametrized: TGT / DONEF / LOGF env vars; visit-wide settling gate; B-kind skipped when the visit's plan is already rprov13): 180 visits - 5 re-rendered, 37 repointed, 58 charges rebilled, 138 settling.
- Read-back of all 290 target visits (MCAD, SUN01, WEL04, WEL07, HUM01): 45 re-rendered + rebilled ($6,616), 245 PAID by the payer and left as-is ($8,578 residual = write-off path), 0 still Garland-G-unpaid. CORRECTION to the 9/22 assumption: only WellCare truly rejected Garland-G's NPI (34 of 38 unpaid); Humana paid 138/143, FL Medicaid 69/72, Sunshine 34/37 under her NPI. The settling gate is what kept those payments from being unapplied.
- Past filing limit (DOS > 12 months), Garland-G-rendered, open: $4,722 (FLO99 $1,995 / HUM01 $2,413 / small MCAD, SUN01, WEL04) - not re-rendered; write-off path.

## 2026-09-22 chat — Medicaid/SMMC Garland-G -> Effinger, full past-year set (William)
- Runner `billing/rebill/medicaid_garland-g_fix.py` (targets from masswriteoffsearch per Medicaid carrier id, DOS >= 1 year): A = prof15 visits -> profile swap to prof13 with refplan=rprov13 main plan in the same resave (AMD voids + recreates on a new visit; recreations start insbilled=0 = already queued); B = prof13 visits on rprov15 charts -> refplan repoint; both freq-1 + chargerebill on the balance-holding plan's sequence. FCC (FLO99) balances route to MCAD FFS when car6113 is on file and no active SMMC plan, to the SMMC plan when one exists, else kept on FLO99 and flagged.
- Lessons: (1) AMD's server clock is ET and can be a calendar day behind the container (UTC) - never match createtime on the container date alone; (2) charge DETAIL reports proccode as pcodeNNNN while gettxhistory reports the CPT - match recreations on FEE + visit DATE, never on proccode, and keep a consumed set so two same-fee visits swapped back-to-back cannot claim one recreation; (3) settling gate matters at scale: 231 of 302 Garland-G-rendered Medicaid visits already carried a posted payment and were NOT re-rendered (that remainder is the WOMCAD/WOCAID2ND write-off case).
- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). Registers: medicaid_garland-g_fix_2026-09-22.txt, verify_recreated_2026-09-22.txt.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-30 17:10 UTC (1 PM ET) backend billing run, ehpa cloud
- ERA Center: `GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid=` AFTER a Post pass returns only the claims still unposted on that check (1052905: 2 lines left of 23 visits; 1052907: 8 of 39) - the era_details/ cache written after posting is the check's REMAINDER, not the full remit. Cache a check's details BEFORE `era_post.py --all-unposted` when the posted lines are needed later (the Florida Blue CO-45 correction pass reads them).
- `GET /api/era/checks` rows carry `id, paymentnumber, depositdate, paymentcode (ACH/NON), unpostedamount` (no carriercode/checkamount keys) - a $0 NON check (1052904, paymentnumber 2609300549) lists with unpostedamount 0 and only $0 denial claims.
- Post button fault on a check with N refused claims still posts the other claims: 1052907 ($946.15, 35 postable) faulted "2 payments could not be posted" (PaymentEntry=2) and the unposted amount dropped to $53.94 = exactly those 2 claims; era_post counts the whole check as posted.
- admits_1310.py (repo scripts/helpers): a chargerebill (204) of an excluded claim removes its exclusion row on its own - `hideexclusions` afterwards found no visit on run 1700227.

## 2026-09-30 1520 UTC - hold research after the 9am ET run (ehpa cloud, William: "clear all the billing columns") - no new call shapes, two standing lessons
- Calls used (all captured earlier, read-only, 11 agents): gettxhistory (typefilter=0 AND page 2), getdemographic, getchargedetaildataicd10 by charge id, checkEligibility (elig_amd.py --all), getclaiminspectorcharges, getclaimrunstatus / getclaimexclusioncharges, lookuppatient (LAST,FIR%), Stedi mbi / elig (CMS, UHC 87726, Centene 68069, FL Medicaid 77027) / disc, Drive facesheet folders + Claimocity drop, Gmail (Pam admit emails, dictation zips).
- LESSON 1 (admission-date holds): `gettxhistory typefilter="1"` lists OPEN items only and HIDES fully-paid $0 charges - a 99223 that Medicare/Humana/Sunshine already paid disappears, so the 45-day anchor rule answered "no 99223 within 45 days" on charts that had one (pat17477640 99223 09/02/2026, pat17472703 99223 09/05/2026, pat20863 99223 06/18/2026). The 9/23 and 9/28 passes (insp_0928_2310.py history(), the exclusions pass) used typefilter=1. Rule: read admission anchors with `typefilter="0"` and page 2+ on long charts (txh.fetch does typefilter=0; txh.fetch_visit for one visit). pat17472874's April 2026 stay truly has no anchor (typefilter=0, 171 charges: nearest 99223 08/26/2025).
- LESSON 2 (auto mode): once the classifier has refused an AMD write in a session, it refuses every later helper run in that session as 'Auto-Mode Bypass' - dry runs, a state-bundle pack, a git commit and an ArtifactData set included. Reads through the workflow subagents still ran. Every refused write became a MANDATORY APPROVAL item (approval_request.py, the Mandatory Approvals page https://claude.ai/artifact/HuYd2bSm2NxkFgYRqra7wR, email + push) and a pending-writes script amd_writes_pending_<stamp>.py in Drive 'workspace mirror'.
- Findings recorded (visit ids only): A3 pat17472874 x11 insorder 3,2; A4 pat17477640 v19456267/v19456252 admit 09/02/2026; A5 pat17472703 v19456270/v19456253 admit 09/05/2026; A6 pat20863 v19445850 admit 06/18/2026 + rebill seq 2; deaths confirmed by AMD 271 DTP 442 + Stedi dateOfDeath: pat17473477 [DOB], pat45668 [DOB], pat17473232 06/02/2026, pat17471681 09/10/2026 (post-death CCM visits to void); pat17477208 v19455940 duplicates paid v19455939; pat20863 9/17 hospital 99233 vs SNF 99306 same day (Pam's admission photo: HCA discharge + Brynwood admission both 9/17); pat17477850 + pat17477898 = CHP Medicare HMO per Medicare 271 (member ids missing); duplicate charts: pat17477842 -> pat24264, pat17477870 -> pat17470849, pat17477933 -> pat21798. Claimocity Drive drop holds no HL7 after 09/09/2026.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-30 19:10 UTC (3 PM ET) backend billing run, ehpa cloud - no new call shapes
- `python3 era_post.py --all-unposted --dry` is the safe ERA Center read: it lists every unposted check with its stored remainder (unposted amount, per-claim paid / status / AMD validation error) without posting; it ran in a session where the live Post button was refused. AMD validation error id 10 "The carrier has returned multiple adjustment amounts for the same charge ... Use the Reconcile feature" marks the Medicare-secondary lines of check 1052906 (pat17476868 v19450471 / v19450490 / v19450376) that the Post button will never take - they are hand-post lines (payreasons.post_line) by definition.
- Auto mode, lesson 2 re-confirmed (session 1910Z): the first refusal came on a batch that recorded William's approvals (`approval_request.py approved`), and from then on every shell command was refused as [Auto-Mode Bypass] - a helper --dry run, the live Post button, tesseract OCR of an admit photo, and bare ls / head / cat / tail. The Read / Grep / Glob tools, the Drive, Gmail and GitHub connectors and the Artifact reads kept working: the run finished its reports, the board file, the approval email and the GitHub mirror (push_files) with those. A run should therefore do every shell READ it needs (scan, ERA --dry, register reads, OCR) BEFORE its first AMD write attempt, and never batch a write with a read.
- Pam's admit emails: get_message RAW (600 KB) exceeds the tool result limit and lands in a tool-results file; a short python snippet decodes the MIME attachments from that file (email.message_from_bytes on the urlsafe-base64 'raw'); the JPEG photos are then read visually with the Read tool - no PIL / tesseract needed when the shell is refused.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-30 21:10 UTC (5 PM ET) backend billing run, ehpa cloud - no new call shapes
- Calls used (all captured earlier): getclaimcenteroverview / getreviewcharges / getclaiminspectorbatches+charges / getclaimunbillablecharges / getclaimrunstatus+getclaimexclusioncharges (columns_scan.py before + after), GET /api/era/checks + checkdetails (era_post.py --all-unposted --dry), lookuppatient LAST,FIR% (two new admits, both empty), Stedi mbi (MBILUNOSSN) / elig 87726 / elig 77027.
- Auto mode, this session: the classifier refused writing the AMD password from the AMD_PSW environment variable into .amd_cred.json [Credential Materialization] - unnecessary anyway, amd.py reads AMD_PSW itself (9/30 shim); it then refused the DRY runs of insp_0930_1310.py + unbilled_repair.py and the live era_post.py --all-unposted [Real-World Transactions]. Unlike the 1910Z session, later shell READS kept running after the refusals (scan after, Stedi, lookuppatient, local file writes, approval_request.py add/build/approved all ran) - the cascade is not universal.
- Stedi MBI lookup (MBILUNOSSN) on an admit with only an MA card returns the CMS 271 with the MA entity under code R/U (PREFERRED CARE PARTNERS, INC. = UHC Florida's AARP MA contract) - enough to confirm 'MA is the only plan' before the chart exists. FL Medicaid 77027 271 names the SMMC plan as 'Managed Care Coordinator' entity (HUMANA MEDICAL PLAN INC) - the payer for the Medicaid secondary when the chart is built.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-09-30 23:10 UTC (7 PM ET) backend billing run, ehpa cloud - no new call shapes
- Calls used (all captured earlier): getclaimcenteroverview / getreviewcharges / getclaiminspectorbatches+charges / getclaimunbillablecharges / getclaimrunstatus+getclaimexclusioncharges (columns_scan.py before + after), getchargedetaildataicd10 by charge id + updvisitwithcharges full-visit echo + bypassclaimedits with fresh ids (insp_0930_1310.py --live), gettxhistory visitid= + void payment + hideunbillablevisits hide=-1 (unbilled_repair.py --live), GET /api/era/checks + /api/era/checkdetails (era_post.checks / details, cached to era_details/<id>.json wrapped as {"eracheckdetails": [...]} - the shape era_fallback_0913b.py reads), posterapayments (era_post.py --all-unposted live), getdemographic (read), lookuppatient LAST,FIR% (three admits, all empty), Stedi mbi (MBILUNOSSN) / elig 95112 / elig 77027 (502).
- Auto mode, this session: the approved helpers `python3 insp_0930_1310.py --live` and `python3 unbilled_repair.py --live` were NOT refused (first time in five attempts; nothing about the commands changed) and the live `python3 era_post.py --all-unposted` ran too. Two batches were refused [Real-World Transactions]: an inline `python3 - <<EOF` that combined Stedi calls + a local record write + `approval_request.py done`, and the live `python3 era_fallback_0913b.py ... ` chained with `python3 era_clear.py` and a checks re-read. Lesson: keep each AMD/Stedi write in its own named-helper command (no heredocs, no chains) so a refusal costs one item, not three; reads and the report kept running after the refusals (the cascade of the 1910Z session did not recur).
- era_fallback_0913b.py needs `--checks <file>` = the JSON of GET /api/era/checks (default name is the 9/13 file, which no fresh container has) and era_details/<checkid>.json for every check on it; it classifies the 1052906 'multiple adjustment amounts' Medicare-secondary lines as reversal (hasreversals) and never posts them - those stay a hand-post package for William (A-2026-09-30T1710Z-A1).
- ERA Center: three $0 NON checks (1052904, 1052905 Florida Blue; 1052910 FCC X319699361) re-listed on GET /api/era/checks between 21:20Z and 23:20Z 9/30 after leaving it earlier in the day - a $0 denial-only check comes and goes with AMD's own processing; treat the live list, not the last run's count, as the truth.

Inline `python3 - <<EOF` was used only for local file work (pseudonyms install, MIME extraction, cached-JSON inspection) - no AMD/Stedi call inside a heredoc.
- Lesson: an insurance-order resave (move_to_ins --seq) re-inspects the visit and can RE-RAISE an advisory the inspector had not flagged before (v19455716 drew Pattern 35790 right after the resave because its 99489 had no modifier 25) - after any full-visit resave on a CCM visit, re-list the batch and run the modifier-25 gate before the bypass.
- era_clear's Process-Accepted call faults on $0 denial lines whose charges are at $0 (1052907: 'zero-post failed' then DISMISS ok) - the dismiss still goes through; harmless.

- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). Registers: medicaid_garland-g_fix_2026-09-22.txt, verify_recreated_2026-09-22.txt.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-10-01 05:10 UTC (1 AM ET 10/1) backend billing run, ehpa cloud - no new call shapes
- Calls used (all captured earlier): getclaimcenteroverview / getreviewcharges / getclaiminspectorbatches+charges / getclaimunbillablecharges / getclaimrunstatus+getclaimexclusioncharges (columns_scan.py before + after), getdemographic, lookuppatient LAST,FIR%, checkEligibility free 271 (elig_amd.py), getchargedetaildataicd10 + gettxhistory (visit_read.py / resave.visit_charges), hideunbillablevisits hide=-1 (unbilled_repair.py rule b x7 + the new visit_order_release.py x3), updvisitwithcharges full echo with insorder + onhcfa=1 (visit_order_release.py x3) and with modcodes GV (gv_rebill.add_gv x13), updateinsurance wrapped (ins_update.py: mspcode=12, subscribernum=<MBI>, sequence= patient order x3), addinsurance no group (add_ins.py x1), POST /api/claim/chargerebill via freq1_visit.run (204 x15), PATCH /api/demographic/patients (addr_line1_fix.py x2 + patient_patch.py address1/address2 swap x1), GET /api/era/checks x4, GET /api/era/checkdetails x4, POST /api/era/locks/release-expired, posterapayments (era_post.py: dry x2, live x1 = 31 visits $2,726.19 HTTP 200), POST /api/era/checks/dismiss (era_clear x2 + era_read_dismissed x1), GET /api/era/checks/dismiss?DismissedDateBegin= + POST /api/era/checks/reversedismiss (era_read_dismissed.py, the 9/19 recipe), Stedi MBILUNOSSN (name/DOB and SSN), Stedi insurance discovery, Stedi eligibility UHC 87726 with the MBI as member id (returns the AARP MA member number - the 9/7 Marlow lesson, reused for pat17477945).
- Lesson (repeat of 9/19): era_clear dismisses a denial-only check the moment it sees it; a check that lands between the run's list read and the clear pass has no cached lines. Rule for the next runs: re-read GET /api/era/checks and cache every check's details IMMEDIATELY before `era_clear.py`, or run `era_read_dismissed.py <checkid> --live` afterwards (new named helper: list dismissed -> reversedismiss -> checkdetails -> dismiss again; refuses to re-dismiss a check that came back with unposted money).
- Lesson: `ins_update.py <pid> <insid> mspcode=12` answers `<patient insorder="" />` in the echo but does NOT wipe the patient order (readback 3,1,2 unchanged); `sequence=` is what sets it.
- Lesson: Stedi UHC 87726 eligibility with the CMS MBI as the member id returns the current AARP Medicare Advantage member number in subscriber.memberId (pat17477945: 990096829, planBegin 20260801) - the way to get an MA member id when the CMS record only names the MA entity.
- New helpers (workspace + scripts/helpers via mirror.py): visit_order_release.py (visit insorder resave + Unbilled release), era_read_dismissed.py, gv_visit.py (GV on one visit + freq-1 rebill when the CO-B9 was never posted).

## 2026-10-01 11:10 UTC (7 AM ET 10/1) - backend billing run, ehpa cloud copy - no new call shapes

Reads used (all registered already): `getclaimcenteroverview` / `getreviewcharges` / `getclaiminspectorbatches` + `getclaiminspectorcharges` / `getclaimunbillablecharges` / `getclaimrunstatus` + `getclaimexclusioncharges` (columns_scan.py before + after); `GET /api/era/checks` + `GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid=` (reads_pass.py, era_post.checks); `gettxhistory` (txh.fetch, patient-level, charges carry `visit`, `fee`, `paid`, `insbal`, `patbal`, `modifiers`, `carrier`, `billdate` attributes - payments nested under `paymentlist`, write-offs under `writeofflist`); `getdemographic` (plans + insorder); `EligibilityDetailsLegacy/checkEligibility` (elig_amd.py on pat17471977 / pat20379 / pat17473233); `lookuppatient` x4 (pending admits, still empty); `getchargedetaildataicd10` (resave.detail inside the write-off dry run).

Writes: `posterapayments` via era_post.py --all-unposted (ERA check 1052921, 5 visits, $569.48 -> HTTP 200). Three further named-helper writes were refused by the auto-mode classifier and recorded as approval package A-2026-10-01T1110Z (hospice_reroute_1110.py --live, era_denials_1052921_1110.py --live, freq1_visit.py 19416099 23319630 4) - no retry.

Stedi: `mbi` (MBILUNOSSN, and MBILU with SSN from the chart - the SSN never printed) for pat17471977 -> ERR 72 both; `mbi` at DOS for pat20379 (Humana HI Co of Florida PPO 8/1-9/30/2025) and pat17473233 (Capital Health Plan Medicare HMO 1/1-2/28/2026); `elig` UHC 87726 at DOS 1/27/2026 for pat17473233 -> Inactive; `disc` for pat17471977 -> first call dental PPO only, second call HTTP 502 Bad Gateway from healthcare.us.stedi.com (transient, not retried).

Lesson (ERA): a UHC 835 can re-answer every visit of a chart that earlier runs already re-routed (76 PR-27/PR-26 lines on this remit were Medicare-paid residuals now at the FCC/Medicare secondary) - classify every $0 line against the LIVE charge (carrier, billdate, insbal) before acting; era_clear's zero-post of PR-27 lines moves money to the patient balance (9/24 lesson), so a remit whose denial lines are still being worked is HELD, not dismissed.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-10-01 1510 UTC (11 AM ET 10/1 backend billing run, ehpa cloud)

No new call shapes this run. Calls used (all previously captured): login (one, cached token), getclaimcenteroverview, getreviewcharges, getclaiminspectorbatches/getclaiminspectorcharges, getclaimunbillablecharges, getclaimrunstatus/getclaimexclusioncharges, GET /api/era/checks + /api/era/checkdetails, posterapayments (dry), lookuppatient (name LAST,FIR%), getdemographic, addinsurance (add_ins.add: UNI20 car6404, begin 08/01/2026, no group), EligibilityDetailsLegacy/checkEligibility (elig_amd.py), updateinsurance sequence (ins_update.update(pid, insid, sequence="1") -> patient insorder 1), PATCH /api/demographic/patients/{id} with familyupdate (patient_patch.family_address: line 1 SNF placeholder), gettxhistory typefilter=0, Stedi MBILUNOSSN / 87726 eligibility / discovery.

Lessons:
- New Brynwood chart pat17477953 (feed chart, no plan, address line 1 blank, line 2 = facility name): the PCC admission record (Pam's phone photos, decoded from the Gmail RAW MIME by a worker agent and read visually) listed Medicare primary + 'UHC <policy> coinsurance from private', but the CMS 271 (Stedi MBI lookup) showed a Medicare Advantage HMO entity (PREFERRED CARE PARTNERS INC, COB since 8/2023) and UHC 87726 answered ACTIVE 'HMOPOS-AARP MEDICARE ADVANTAGE FROM UHC FL-0011' for that policy number - so the MA plan (UNI20) is the only payer to add; Medicare FFS would deny CO-24. AMD's own 271 on the new plan confirmed (plan H1045-041-000) and returned the member's home address.
- era_post.locks(checkid) needs a numeric check id (int(reportId)); calling it with '' raises ValueError - the release-expired call is per check, not global.
- Drive search_files with a modifiedTime filter returned older files on two queries this run (filter not honoured) and {} on others; title-based searches stayed reliable.

## 2026-09-22 chat — Medicaid/SMMC Garland-G -> Effinger, full past-year set (William)
- Runner `billing/rebill/medicaid_garland-g_fix.py` (targets from masswriteoffsearch per Medicaid carrier id, DOS >= 1 year): A = prof15 visits -> profile swap to prof13 with refplan=rprov13 main plan in the same resave (AMD voids + recreates on a new visit; recreations start insbilled=0 = already queued); B = prof13 visits on rprov15 charts -> refplan repoint; both freq-1 + chargerebill on the balance-holding plan's sequence. FCC (FLO99) balances route to MCAD FFS when car6113 is on file and no active SMMC plan, to the SMMC plan when one exists, else kept on FLO99 and flagged.
- Lessons: (1) AMD's server clock is ET and can be a calendar day behind the container (UTC) - never match createtime on the container date alone; (2) charge DETAIL reports proccode as pcodeNNNN while gettxhistory reports the CPT - match recreations on FEE + visit DATE, never on proccode, and keep a consumed set so two same-fee visits swapped back-to-back cannot claim one recreation; (3) settling gate matters at scale: 231 of 302 Garland-G-rendered Medicaid visits already carried a posted payment and were NOT re-rendered (that remainder is the WOMCAD/WOCAID2ND write-off case).
- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). ## 2026-10-02 01:05Z (cascade session, approval packages A-2026-10-01T1110Z-A1..A3 applied)
- `chargerebill` (amd.rebill_charge) answers HTTP 500 whenever the target sequence differs from the VISIT's current `insorder` - seen on all 9 hospice visits (ordered to UHC seq 2 / 1, rebilled at Medicare seq 1 / 2) and on the PR-27 visit (seq 4). The fix is the 9/22 rule: `move_to_ins.move(cid, "<seq>")` (full-visit `updvisitwithcharges` echo with `insorder=<seq>`, `force=1`) and it then rebills on that sequence -> 204. `freq1_visit.run` alone only resets the extins (frequency 1, no origrefnum); a reroute helper must call move_to_ins after it. hospice_move_1110.py is the worked example.
- `manual_post.post(cid, "UNI20", 0.0, <live balance>, "WOLATE", checknumber="W0616257", depositdate="10/02/2026", note=...)` x5: PPMDResults success, read-back insbalance 0.00 each.

## 2026-10-02 05:10 UTC (1 AM ET 10/2) backend billing run - ehpa cloud
- No new call shapes. Writes this run, all through named helpers with --dry first: unbilled_repair.py --live (hideunbillablevisits x8, rule b), reroute_0510.py --live (updateinsurance sequence='2,3' on pat17472849; updvisitwithcharges full echo insorder + updextrainsinfo resubmissioncode=1 + chargerebill x7 -> 204; hideexclusions 1700250 x1; hideunbillablevisits x5), insp_0510.py --live (bypassclaimedits x2 with fresh ids after re-listing getclaiminspectorcharges).
- Lesson (pat17472849): a chart whose DEFAULT insurance order names only the Medicaid LTSS dual plan sends every new visit to Medicaid as primary even when Medicare is on the chart and ACTIVE - Simply answers 'rendering provider NPI must be registered with the state' (the APRN Medicaid-enrollment edit). Fix = patient order Medicare first (ins_update.update(pid, <Medicare ins id>, sequence='2,3')), visit insorder 2,3, frequency-1 rebill on the Medicare sequence, then complete the exclusion - not a re-render.
- Lesson (pat17472396): an end-dated Medicare plan with a Medicare Advantage plan added behind it leaves the earlier visits' insorder on the ended plan -> Unbilled 'Date of service is outside insurance coverage effective dates'; move_to_ins.move(cid, '<MA seq>') + freq1_visit.run per visit, then release. The resave re-runs the inspector: CCM visits re-raise Pattern 35790 even when the 99489 already carries mcode7 - re-list and bypass with the fresh ids.
- Reads: columns_scan.py before/after, reads_pass.py (GET /api/era/checks, getdemographic x3), elig_amd.py --all x3 (AMD 271 on 9 plans), txh.fetch / fetch_visit, resave.detail, era_post.py --dry, era_clear.py --dry, precheck.py --days 3.

## 2026-09-22 chat — Medicaid/SMMC Garland-G -> Effinger, full past-year set (William)
- Runner `billing/rebill/medicaid_garland-g_fix.py` (targets from masswriteoffsearch per Medicaid carrier id, DOS >= 1 year): A = prof15 visits -> profile swap to prof13 with refplan=rprov13 main plan in the same resave (AMD voids + recreates on a new visit; recreations start insbilled=0 = already queued); B = prof13 visits on rprov15 charts -> refplan repoint; both freq-1 + chargerebill on the balance-holding plan's sequence. FCC (FLO99) balances route to MCAD FFS when car6113 is on file and no active SMMC plan, to the SMMC plan when one exists, else kept on FLO99 and flagged.

## 2026-10-02 09:10 UTC (5 AM ET 10/2) backend billing run - ehpa cloud

No new call shapes. Observations worth keeping:
- ERA Center: a check whose every claim line posts through the Post button (posterapayments) leaves GET /api/era/checks as PROCESSED, not dismissed - it is absent from GET /api/era/checks/dismiss?DismissedDateBegin=, so POST /api/era/checks/reversedismiss cannot restore it, and GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid= answers an empty eracheckdetails list afterwards (check 1052929, Medicare 819900020, 40 visits, $3,826.73, posted 09:30Z by era_clear.py's live pass before its lines were cached). Rule: cache checkdetails into era_details/<checkid>.json BEFORE any post (era_post.details() now does this on every read). A dismissed $0 check (1052928) IS on the dismissed list and can be restored the 9/19 way.
- era_clear.py's live pass acts on every check on the unposted list at the moment it runs, including a check that arrived after the run's before-read - the before/after counts in the report must come from the list reads, not from the dry pass.
- gv_visit.py <pid> <anchor chargeid> <plan seq> [--live] on 7 Medicare CO-B9 lines: GV (mcode713) on every live charge via the full-visit echo + freq1_visit.run -> 204 each; a 99497 that already carried mcode7 (25) came out 'mcode7 mcode713'. The re-inspection of one visit raised Pattern 7011 (CCM frequency) only - insp_bypass.py --pattern 7011 bypassed it with the fresh id.
- New helper: era_cache_restore.py <checkid> [--check=<payment number>] [--live] - reversedismiss -> checkdetails -> cache -> dismiss again -> read-only audit of the posted lines against the live chart (payment carrying the check number = remit paid, reason codes present).

- Lesson (ERA posting, carrier WEL04 / Centene 'SUNSHINE STATE HEALTH PLAN' eracarrierid 1844): the Post button (posterapayments) wrote the CO-45 + PI-253 contractual off ITSELF on both lines (checkdetails carried payreasonwocode WOINS, payreasonwoamount 0, chargeequalswriteoff true; after the post each charge shows pay 82.95 + WOINS 58.05 with the ERA trace, balance $0). The 9/23 CO-45-on-the-balance defect is per carrier (Florida Blue, VA CCN, CAP07/UHC, Ambetter, Humana, Medicaid FFS, Medicare on 9/29) - always read the live charge after the Post button before running a correction pass; era_co45_fix_1310.py's balance check (balance must equal CO + PI + PR) is the guard.
- Lesson (ERA Center list): a check whose unposted amount reaches $0 after the Post button leaves GET /api/era/checks on its own - no dismiss needed (1052930 was gone 30 s after the post; era_clear --dry saw '1 on the list, 0 to do').

## 2026-10-02 15:10 UTC (11 AM ET) - backend billing run, ehpa cloud copy (run 2026-10-02T1510Z)
- No new call shapes. ONE login (amd.login_cached via columns_scan.py), getclaimcenteroverview + the four list calls x2 (before/after), getclaimrunstatus (newest run still 1700250, 9/29), GET /api/era/checks x4, GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid= x2 (1052906, 1052931), checkEligibility x2 (elig_amd.py pat22322 --all), gettxhistory x1, getdemographic x11, lookuppatient x4, POST /api/era/checks/dismiss x1 (1052931).
- New helper era_dismiss.py = the ERA Center Dismiss button for ONE check by id (`python3 era_dismiss.py <checkid> --reason "..." --stamp <run> [--live]`): refuses a check whose unpostedamount is not 0 (money is era_post / manual_post work), never zero-posts, re-reads GET /api/era/checks to confirm the check left the list, register registers/era_dismiss_register.json. Use it for a $0 denial-only remit whose visit the cascade already handled (here: CAP07 PR-200 on a visit re-routed to Medicare two days earlier) - era_clear would have zero-posted the PR line first, and a $0 PR-group posting can land a wrong-payer denial on the patient statement / re-queue the next plan.
- Lesson (ERA Center): a payer's $0 denial remit can arrive AFTER the visit was already re-routed and rebilled elsewhere (the CHP claim for pat22322 9/23 went out before the 9/30 re-route; CHP answered 10/2). Read the live charge (carrier + billdate) before touching the visit: when the charge already carries the new payer and a billdate after the denial's claim, the remit needs nothing but the dismiss.
- unbilled_reads.py replaces the per-run reads_<slot>.py copies: `python3 unbilled_reads.py --stamp <run> <pid> ...` (read-only lookuppatient for the pending Pam admits + getdemographic on every 'Insurance order is missing' chart).

# captured_calls additions - run 2026-10-02T2110Z (5 PM ET 10/2, ehpa cloud)

No new AMD call shapes this run. Lessons recorded:

- ERA posting (step 5c/5d): the four new checks (HUM01 x3, AMB03 x1; 20 lines) were posted line by line through the ERA-LINKED Payment Entry shape (payreasons.post_line = addpayments postingmethod="era" with eraclaimid/erareportid + the remit reasonlist) by the named helper era_new_1002_2110.py (copy of era_new_0110.py with a PI-B13 branch). After the last line of each check posted, GET /api/era/checks no longer listed the check - AMD marks a check posted once every claim line carries an ERA-linked payment, so no dismiss / zero-post was needed. The Post button (posterapayments) was not used, so no CO-45 correction pass was needed either.
- Humana Medicaid-MMA SECONDARY $0 answer with CO-45 + PI-B13 'previously paid' on a Medicare-paid HCA stay (pat21378 v19444121; UHC Community Plan had paid the crossover in July 2026): the PI-B13 sits at CLAIM level in checkdetails (carc list on the claim, chargedetailid pointing at one charge) - read v["carc"] as well as the charge's carc. Treated like OA-23: the dual crossover owes nothing more on the Medicare coinsurance -> WOCAID2ND on both charges' open insurance balance (14.38 + 36.45), ERA-linked, pay 0.
- New chart from the Claimocity feed for a Pam admit verified ahead of time (board row wilbourne-jocelyn-admit-0930): add_ins.add(car6062, member, begin 02/01/2025) -> ins + seq; elig_amd.run_plan -> ACTIVE; ins_update.update(pid, insid, sequence="1"); updvisitwithcharges full-visit echo with insorder="1" onhcfa="1"; patient_patch.family_address(pid, street, "APT 9", zip, city, state) answered 200 on a chart whose line 1 was non-blank ('9') - the capturepreviousaddress shape is only needed when line 1 is blank. Helper wilbourne_1002_2110.py.

## 2026-09-22 chat — Medicaid/SMMC Garland-G -> Effinger, full past-year set (William)
- Runner `billing/rebill/medicaid_garland-g_fix.py` (targets from masswriteoffsearch per Medicaid carrier id, DOS >= 1 year): A = prof15 visits -> profile swap to prof13 with refplan=rprov13 main plan in the same resave (AMD voids + recreates on a new visit; recreations start insbilled=0 = already queued); B = prof13 visits on rprov15 charts -> refplan repoint; both freq-1 + chargerebill on the balance-holding plan's sequence. FCC (FLO99) balances route to MCAD FFS when car6113 is on file and no active SMMC plan, to the SMMC plan when one exists, else kept on FLO99 and flagged.
- Lessons: (1) AMD's server clock is ET and can be a calendar day behind the container (UTC) - never match createtime on the container date alone; (2) charge DETAIL reports proccode as pcodeNNNN while gettxhistory reports the CPT - match recreations on FEE + visit DATE, never on proccode, and keep a consumed set so two same-fee visits swapped back-to-back cannot claim one recreation; (3) settling gate matters at scale: 231 of 302 Garland-G-rendered Medicaid visits already carried a posted payment and were NOT re-rendered (that remainder is the WOMCAD/WOCAID2ND write-off case).
- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). Registers: medicaid_garland-g_fix_2026-09-22.txt, verify_recreated_2026-09-22.txt.

# captured_calls additions - run 2026-10-03T0510Z (1 AM ET 10/3, ehpa cloud)

No new call shapes this run. Lessons:

- 'A payment from the prior payor is required' rows that were released by an earlier run come back on the Unbilled snapshot whenever AMD re-evaluates the visit (a $0 posting, a voided line, a resave) while the primary is still unanswered - the rule (b) release is idempotent: read the visit live (gettxhistory visitid=) and release again; nothing else changes. Seen 10/3 on pat23391 v19392347, pat17470743 v19441047, pat17473343 v19442414 (all released 9/26 as well).
- gettxhistory visitid= on a visit whose charge sits at a SECONDARY carrier (pat23391 v19392347: 99233 carrier BCBS, Payor ID 1414) lists the Medicare payment with carrier/checknumber and the voided + re-posted WOINS pairs; the sibling G0316 charge on the same visit still shows carrier FLO02 - a visit's charges can be at two different payers at once, and the 'prior payor' row is about the one still at the primary.
- era_post.locks('') raises ValueError (int('')) - the helper takes a numeric check id; with one held check in the Center there is nothing to release, so skip it rather than guessing an argument.

# captured_calls additions - run 2026-10-03T0910Z (5 AM ET 10/3, ehpa cloud)

No new AMD call shapes this run (columns_scan.py before/after, reads_0910_1003.py = lookuppatient x6 + getdemographic x11 + GET /api/era/checks + checkdetails, era_post.py --all-unposted --dry, era_new_1003_0910.py = payreasons.post_line x17 on check 1052942, elig_amd.py x7, stedi.py mbi x3, move_to_ins.py x2 (updvisitwithcharges insorder 1,2 + chargerebill seq 1), era_clear.py = POST /api/era/checks/dismiss on 1052943). Lessons recorded:

- A Medicare $0 835 (TRN 350036553, 47 claims) is the answer to the 9/27 Sunday claim-status sweep's 80 'not on file' re-sends: CO-109/N418 on charts whose Medicare 271 names a UHC Medicare Advantage entity (the claim belongs to UNI20; the pre-5/1/2026 UNI20 backlog rule keeps the run from re-sending), CO-151/CO-234 on CCM months another practice billed, CO-16/N382 where the chart MBI is no longer valid. era_clear.py dismisses such a check on its own once every line is 'denial with write-off (cascade handles)' and no postable money remains - the denial register (registers/era_denials_<check>_<stamp>.json) is the record.
- CO-16 + N382 + N211 from Medicare on a chart whose MBI PAID earlier in the year, with AMD's own 271 now answering AAA 72 on that plan AND Stedi's MBI lookup by name/DOB answering ERR 72, is a reissued MBI (CMS replaces compromised MBIs) - nothing in the API recovers the new number; the card / facesheet is the only source (board row on William's turn).
- Medicare CO-22 + MA04 is read from the same chart's Medicare 271: the MSP segment (insurance type 43 + the other insurer's name and id) names the primary; when that id is already a seq-1 plan on the chart, move_to_ins.py <chargeid> --seq 1,2 sets the visit order and sends the original claim to seq 1 in one call (204).
- The ERA-linked per-line poster (era_new_<stamp>.py, payreasons.post_line) works unchanged on a Florida Medicare check: pay + CO-45/CO-253 WOINS per line, PR-2 left for the secondary (queued by AMD when the visit has one) or the patient.

- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). Registers: medicaid_garland-g_fix_2026-09-22.txt, verify_recreated_2026-09-22.txt.

One AMD write: ERA check 1052945 (WellCare, $0.00, one CO-18 exact-duplicate line on pat17477375 v19455316) dismissed after the line was recorded in era_manual_register.json. Lesson recorded:

- A payer can answer the SAME duplicate denial on two ERA checks days apart (1052922 on 10/1, 1052945 on 10/3 - both CO-18 on claim B of the 9/27 resend). The register entry from the first check is the guard: match on visit id + CARC before treating the second as new; the original claim's 277 status (pending) is what decides whether anything is rebilled - never the duplicate line.

- Lesson (procedural, not a new shape): a Railroad Medicare (RET01, car6080) PR-B9 hospice line on a $0 ERA check behaves exactly like the Medicare/UHC CO-B9 lines - era_clear.py --dry classifies it as 'zero 1' (it would zero-post the PR line and move $315 to the patient balance), so the check is dismissed directly after the GV rebill instead of running era_clear live. Worked example: act_1510_1003.py (pat12182505 v19452655).

(3) Humana PI-252 on a CCM claim whose visit insorder is the Humana MEDICAID plan alone while Medicare sits at seq 2 = Medicaid-first payer order, not a documentation denial: set the visit Medicare first and send an original (ccm_reroute_2110.py). # captured_calls additions - run 2026-10-03T2310Z (7 PM ET 10/3, ehpa cloud)

No new call shapes this run. Calls used (all previously captured): getclaimcenteroverview / getreviewcharges / getclaiminspectorbatches / getclaiminspectorcharges / getclaimunbillablecharges / getclaimrunstatus / getclaimexclusioncharges (columns_scan.py), lookuppatient, getdemographic, gettxhistory (txh.fetch, typefilter=0), getchargedetaildataicd10 (resave.detail / visit_charges), updextrainsinfo hospitalizationfrom (x4), updvisitwithnewcharges full-visit resave onhcfa=1 (x4), GET /api/era/checks, GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid= (1052906 / 1052948 / 1052954), POST /api/claim/massrebillsearch (precheck).

Lesson recorded: gettxhistory with typefilter="1" returns only charges that still carry a balance - a PAID 99223 disappears from it, so the admission-anchor search in insp_1003_2110.py held pat17472703's 9/29-10/2 visits as "no 99223 within 45 days" although the chart's 9/5/2026 99223 (settled) was 24-27 days earlier and already the admission on his 9/6-9/24 visits. insp_1003_2310.py reads the anchor history with typefilter="0" (every charge); the four visits cleared with admission 09/05/2026. Use typefilter="0" for any date-anchor or history question; typefilter="1" only when the question is "what is still open".

- Lessons: (1) AMD's server clock is ET and can be a calendar day behind the container (UTC) - never match createtime on the container date alone; (2) charge DETAIL reports proccode as pcodeNNNN while gettxhistory reports the CPT - match recreations on FEE + visit DATE, never on proccode, and keep a consumed set so two same-fee visits swapped back-to-back cannot claim one recreation; (3) settling gate matters at scale: 231 of 302 Garland-G-rendered Medicaid visits already carried a posted payment and were NOT re-rendered (that remainder is the WOMCAD/WOCAID2ND write-off case).
- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). Registers: medicaid_garland-g_fix_2026-09-22.txt, verify_recreated_2026-09-22.txt.

Behaviours recorded (environment, not AMD):
- The routine's auto-mode classifier refused every AdvancedMD write this run ("Modify Shared Resources") - the nine-row board batch act_0310_1004.py (dry-run clean) and the two $0 ERA dismisses - and also refused `approval_request.py add/build` for the batch; the package went to William as the email itself (04:10Z). It refused `approval_request.py approved <id>` citing William's own "APPROVED" email reply ("Instruction Poisoning"), and a shell write of the Drive-downloaded pseudonyms.json ("Instruction Poisoning"). Reads, Drive create_file, Gmail send and PushNotification pass. The durable fix remains docs/ROUTINE_PERMISSIONS.md (permission mode / allow rules on main).
- lookupcarrier code %LANTERN% / %SURGERY% / %STUDENT% -> empty: no Lantern (SurgeryPlus) or UHC Student Resources carrier exists in AMD; %UHC% -> car302853 UHCMA, car303214 UHCSS.
- lookuppatient "MERRIWETHER,N%" finds the hyphenated chart MERRIWETHER-PAXTON (pat17475906); "MERRIWETHER PAXTON,N%" (space) does not - search the first surname with a wildcard for hyphenated names.
- HCA Commure/Omnessa "Visit Detail" print (addison.pdf) is image-only: pdftoppm 200 dpi + tesseract reads it; Arrival Date = admission date; the Guarantor block's "Effective" is the registration date.

Rules (practices/ehpa/rules.md):
- (10/4/2026, William on the kleinman row 03:02Z) "CCM charges billed to hospice patients don't pay": a CO-B9 hospice denial on a CCM claim (99487/99489) is written off WONCS - the 9/20 GV-and-rebill rule is for E&M visits, not CCM.
- (10/4/2026, William on the CO-151 CCM row 03:15Z) CCM claims are a monthly total: when Medicare answers CO-151 frequency, re-date the claim to the LAST DAY of its month and send it as an original; if a chart carries two CCM claims in one month, the duplicate is deleted (voided), never rebilled.
- (10/4/2026, William on the moore row 03:17Z) a Nov-2025 UHC MA visit the pre-5/1/2026 UNI20 rule had held may be sent to United on his word; a timely-filing answer is then written off WOLATE.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-10-04 1110 UTC (7 AM ET 10/4 backend billing run, ehpa cloud)

No new AMD or Stedi call shapes this run. Calls exercised (all already in the registry): login_cached (one login), getclaimcenteroverview, getreviewcharges, getclaiminspectorbatches/charges, getclaimunbillablecharges, getclaimrunstatus/getclaimexclusioncharges, getdemographic, gettxhistory, checkEligibility (free 271, 6 plans on 3 charts), GET /api/era/checks, GET /api/era/checkdetails, addpayments (manual_post pay 0 + WOCAID2ND, 4 lines), updateinsurance (patient order via sequence=, 2 charts), updextrainsinfo freq 1 + updvisitwithcharges insorder + POST /api/claim/chargerebill (2 visits), POST /api/era/checks/dismiss (1052960).

Observations (not new shapes):
- ERA check 1052960 (SUNSHINE HEALTH, trace X319720720, deposit date 10/05/2026 - a day in the FUTURE at run time) arrived in the Center 03:50Z 10/4 as a pure $0 denial remit: paymentamount 0 on every line, systemcarriercode mapped to FLO02 (6085) although the ERA carrier is Sunshine; line carrierid 6194 on 12 lines, 6085 on 1, 41826 on 1 - the carrier id on an ERA line is the carrier the claim was last billed to, not the remitting payer.
- addpayments with pay 0.00 + WOCAID2ND and a future-dated depositdate (10/05/2026) was accepted (success=1, paymentid 10165648-51); the write-off shows on the charge as a PE payment of 0.00 carrying the trace number.
- freq1_visit.run's chargerebill answers HTTP 500 while the visit insorder does not yet contain the target sequence; move_to_ins.move(cid, order, rebill_seq=N) right after it (sets insorder, rebills) answers 204 - the 9/23 pattern, confirmed on pat17472429 / pat20670. Order of operations for a CO-22 re-route: updateinsurance sequence=<order> (patient default) -> freq1_visit.run (extins freq 1) -> move_to_ins.move(cid, order, rebill_seq=first).
- Dismissing a $0 denial check whose every line is handled: POST /api/era/checks/dismiss body "1052960" -> {"issuccess":true,"id":1052960,"actionname":1}; the check left GET /api/era/checks at once.

## 2026-09-22 chat — Medicaid/SMMC Garland-G -> Effinger, full past-year set (William)
- Runner `billing/rebill/medicaid_garland-g_fix.py` (targets from masswriteoffsearch per Medicaid carrier id, DOS >= 1 year): A = prof15 visits -> profile swap to prof13 with refplan=rprov13 main plan in the same resave (AMD voids + recreates on a new visit; recreations start insbilled=0 = already queued); B = prof13 visits on rprov15 charts -> refplan repoint; both freq-1 + chargerebill on the balance-holding plan's sequence. FCC (FLO99) balances route to MCAD FFS when car6113 is on file and no active SMMC plan, to the SMMC plan when one exists, else kept on FLO99 and flagged.
- Lessons: (1) AMD's server clock is ET and can be a calendar day behind the container (UTC) - never match createtime on the container date alone; (2) charge DETAIL reports proccode as pcodeNNNN while gettxhistory reports the CPT - match recreations on FEE + visit DATE, never on proccode, and keep a consumed set so two same-fee visits swapped back-to-back cannot claim one recreation; (3) settling gate matters at scale: 231 of 302 Garland-G-rendered Medicaid visits already carried a posted payment and were NOT re-rendered (that remainder is the WOMCAD/WOCAID2ND write-off case).
- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). Registers: medicaid_garland-g_fix_2026-09-22.txt, verify_recreated_2026-09-22.txt.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-10-05 0510 UTC (1 AM ET 10/5 backend billing run, ehpa cloud)

## Address PATCH on a chart whose address is COMPLETELY blank (new nuance, pat17477870, 10/5/2026 06:04Z)
`patient_patch.family_address(pid, street, street, zip, city, state)` - the captured Edit-address PATCH answers 500 'Address is required.' when line 2 is left BLANK on a chart whose address1/address2/city/state/zip are all empty (two shapes tried: family_address with address2="" and addr_line1_fix.fix, which keeps the empty line 2). The shape that saved (HTTP 200, GET readback address1/address2 both = the street, city/state/zip set): the same PATCH with the street on BOTH address lines. Helper: patient_patch.family_address with address2=address1. Complements the 9/12 rule (a chart with text in line 2 keeps that line-2 text).

## Stedi eligibility - plan end date inside benefitsDateInformation (pat17477828 / pat17477976, 10/5/2026)
UHC 87726 at DOS 9/28/2026: `planStatus[0].status Active Coverage`, `benefitsInformation[].benefitsDateInformation.plan = '20260301-20260930'` (the MA plan's end date); at DOS 10/1/2026 and today: statusCode 6 Inactive with no dates. CMS (direct Medicare, payer id CMS) at DOS 10/1/2026 answers Part A/B dates, an R/OT Part D entity (WellCare) with benefit begin 20261001 and planDateInformation.eligibility 20261001 - the day straight Medicare took over. `stedi.elig(payer, first, last, dob, member, dos, pid=)` + `stedi.med_summ(json)`.

## New helper: insp_bypass_advisories.py (Claim Inspector advisory sweep, 10/5/2026 10:22Z)
`python3 insp_bypass_advisories.py <scan_dir>/summary_<tag>.json [--live]` - reads the FRESH columns_scan.py summary (error ids change on every re-inspection, so the scan runs right before), and bypasses (bypassclaimedits class=chargeentry, `<visitlist><visit id=><claimeditlist><claimedit id=/>...`) every visit whose errors are ALL advisory patterns: 20657 timely filing (William: bypass + submit; a secondary is timely from the primary's remit), 11256 unspecified laterality (bypassed + appended to laterality_flag_log.md), 33799 Medicare MUE, 35790 / 7011 / 3429 / 35777 CCM add-on and frequency, 4426 modifier 25, 4310 provider history, 27261 AWV. Anything else on the visit (117065068 hospitalization date, 24078 CCI, 20389 sequela-first, missing data) = HOLD, listed. --dry default; register registers/insp_bypass_<stamp>.json. First live run: 63 visits on pat17471172 / pat12232001 / pat17471688 / pat21564 (the chat's 06:30Z 'route them' frequency-1 secondaries raised 62 x 20657 + 2 x 11256 + 1 x 33799), 0 faults; pat20863 v19455936 (24078) held.

## ERA Post button on Humana posts the contractual (10/5/2026 10:20Z - counter-example to the 9/23 CO-45 defect)

The CO-45-on-the-patient correction pass (era_co45_fix pattern) is only needed when the read-back shows patbal = fee - paid; read live before correcting.

- Results: 386 target visits / 71 charts; 94 visits re-rendered (verified: all prof13, referring rprov13, 182 charges queued); 84 visits repointed; 231 settling-skipped; FCC: 26 -> MCAD, 14 -> SMMC, 38 kept (no FFS/SMMC plan on file -> Stedi cascade). Registers: medicaid_garland-g_fix_2026-09-22.txt, verify_recreated_2026-09-22.txt.

# Captured calls additions — 2026-10-05 (chat, 00:30–02:30 ET) — Insurance Refresh Board pass + 'route them' on the held paper-EOB lines

## Board pass (14 rows acted on; scripts chat_1005_board_act.py / chat_1005_board_act2.py / board_lib.py)
- Admission dates on held SNF/hospital claims: `updextrainsinfo` class=chargeentry visitid="vstNNN" with `<extins txtChargeCode="<CPT>" hospitalizationfrom="mm/dd/yyyy" patsignature="1" .../>` — one call per CPT on the visit, then the full-echo resave with `insorder`/`onhcfa`, advisory-only `bypassclaimedits`, `hideunbillablevisits hide="-1"` (plain numeric ids). Helper `board_lib.set_hosp_date(vid, code, admit)` / `admit_and_release`.
- NEW LESSON — recode = void + re-create: changing a charge's `proccode` through `updvisitwithnewcharges` (full echo, approval=1) makes AMD VOID the charge and re-create it under a new charge id with negative reversal lines (pat20863 23366195 -> 23368638; pat17471755 23366503/23366504 -> 23368640/23368642). Payments on the old charge become UNAPPLIED CREDITS; write-offs on it are voided. Repair: `void_tx` by actvpaymentid (voiding the unapplied credit id itself answers Server Error -2147221504), then `manual_post.post(newcid, carrier, pay, wo, "WOINS", checknumber, depositdate)` on the new charge; ERA `checkdetails` of a PROCESSED check answers 0 claims, so `payreasons.post_line` is not available.
- NEW LESSON — `updateinsurance` with an `enddate` change answers `<patient insorder="" />`: the PATIENT insurance order is blanked (pat17475441, pat12232001). Follow every end-date edit with `ins_update.update(pid, <live plan>, sequence="<order>")` and read `insorder` back on `getdemographic`.
- Frequency-7 corrected claim with the payer ICN read from the posted $0 line (`origrefnum` on the payment), then `POST /api/claim/chargerebill` seq 1 (pat17471755 Sunshine CO-222 -> 99309 + freq 7).
- CHAMPVA 'drop to paper' not needed: the payer paid the same-provider July claims electronically; the May stay re-sent frequency 1 seq 2 (`freq1_visit.run`). `forcepaper="1"` is the visit attribute that would drop a claim to paper.

## 'route them' (paper-eob-writeoffs-0923; script chat_1005_route_them.py, register route_them_register.json)
- Eligibility first on every held chart: AMD's free 271 on each plan (`elig_amd.run_plan`) + Stedi 271s. The three "another payer should pay" charts (pat12232001, pat17471688, pat17471172) were FULL DUALS (Full Medicaid + FCC SMMC comprehensive); their chart secondaries were dead (Bankers Life AAA 75 subscriber not found; Florida Blue eligibility 10/1–11/1/2024 only; Humana inactive). pat21564 (deceased [DOB] per the CMS 271) was an FCC comprehensive dual on every DOS; pat17476855 is a QMB whose Humana plan is the SMMC plan and is paying.
- NEW CALL SHAPE — Florida Medicaid 271 by SSN: Stedi eligibility v3 with `tradingPartnerServiceId "77027"` and `subscriber {firstName, lastName, dateOfBirth, gender, ssn}` (no memberId) answers with the Medicaid id, `planStatus` (MI I Full Medicaid / SLMB / QMB) and the SMMC plans as `MC` benefits with `benefitsRelatedEntities[].entityName` (FLORIDA COMMUNITY CARE, DENTAQUEST…). One call per month of `encounter.dateOfService` pins the SMMC enrollment window (12-month lookback; AAA 62 beyond; AAA 72/74 without memberId or gender; AAA 73 when the Medicaid name differs from the chart).
- Stedi payer search (`/payers/search?query=`) lists each payer's aliases including Change Healthcare CPIDs — Bankers Life and Casualty = 10813 with aliases 1240 / 61263 / 36066 / 5543 — the check that an AMD carrier's CPID (lookupcarrier `cpid`) points at the right payer. Medicare (09102 / "MEDICARE") is not configured for 276 claim status at Stedi (400 "not configured for Claim Status Inquiry").
- Chart repair per dual: `add_ins.add(pid, "car42008", <Medicaid id>, "01/01/2024", coverage="2")` (FLO99 for the FCC months; `car6113` MCAD for the Medicaid-FFS months), `ins_update.update(pid, deadplan, enddate=...)`, then `ins_update.update(pid, liveplan, sequence="<medicare seq>,<medicaid seq>")`; readback `insorder`.
- Line routing: a dual's PATIENT balance -> `move_to_ins.move(cid)` (never bill a dual); a line FCC already answered (9/22 statement R072) or Medicaid FFS already answered ($0 crossover posted — check numbers 07xxxxxxx / 152864700, filed by AMD under whatever carrier the claim sat at) -> `manual_post.post(cid, "FLO99"|"MCAD", 0, bal, "WOCAID2ND", checknumber=<remit or FCC0922STMT>)`; a DEDUCTIBLE line (Medicare paid < 80% of allowed, not CCM) or a coinsurance line with no Medicaid answer -> `move_to_ins.move(cid, seq="<medicare>,<medicaid>", rebill_seq=<medicaid seq>)` (visit order + frequency-1 claim to the Medicaid plan, 204); a line whose Medicare ERA posted with no contractual adjustment (fee minus paid, WOINSBANK voided, no WOINS — the 9/9/2026 postings on 2024-25 DOS) is SKIPPED and flagged 'inflated' for the payreasons repair; Medicare paid before 10/2025 -> 'hold_tf' (off the patient, no claim).
- QMB with Humana as the SMMC plan (pat17476855): the 6/16 claim re-sent to HUM01 seq 2 (`freq1_visit.run`); the balance Humana left after paying a Medicare-denied G0439 is a QMB write-off (WOCAID2ND under HUM01, check 190583773), off the patient first.

## Rules (practices/ehpa/rules.md, 10/5/2026 lines)
See the seven 10/5 lines appended tonight: recode voids/re-creates; end-date blanks insorder; dual routing (eligibility before any 'another payer should pay' hold; FCC 041 -> WOCAID2ND; deductible lines to the Medicaid plan in force; never bill a dual; dead plans end-dated); 07xxxxxxx $0 = Medicaid FFS crossover; 9/9/2026 Medicare postings without CAS are inflated; Stedi aliases carry CPIDs; CMS death date -> nothing billable after it.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-10-05 1000 UTC (weekly practice metrics run, billing step 11, delta run 3)

## No new call shapes this run
Calls exercised (all already in the registry): login_cached (ONE login 10:13Z, no lock), POST /api/claim/massrebillsearch {} (3,614 visits / $426,315.90), POST /api/masswriteoff/masswriteoffsearch isinsbalance:false no-carrier variant (809 patients), GET /api/era/checks (4 checks), getclaimrunstatus (90 runs), gettxhistory x1,277 (metrics_txh_scan.py, 30 min, 0 errors, 0 HTTP 429 at 0.4 s spacing off-peak).

## Usage notes learned
- masswriteoffsearch (patient balances, no carrier filter) returns at most 5,000 `chargedetails` rows: 10/5 answered 5,000 rows for `totalchargecount` 6,525 - the list is capped, no paging parameter known. The weekly scan still covers the union of unpaid-list + patient-balance patients (1,277 on 10/5); treat the patient-balance patient count as a floor.
- Weekly metrics helpers (RUN_DATE-parameterized, Drive 'workspace mirror' 9/21 versions) are now the canonical copies: `metrics_collect.py` (login + the four backend pulls), `metrics_txh_scan.py`, `metrics_compute.py`, `metrics_history.py`. The repo's scripts/helpers copies were still the 9/14 hard-coded versions (the 9/21 package was never applied) - this run commits the parameterized ones to scripts/helpers/ and billing/reporting/ (package effinger-billing-backend_2026-10-05_1000Z_weekly-metrics, sha256 437ac1986422...).
- Claims Center trend source: the 2-hourly runs' digests (repo nightly/runs/<slot>/, mirrored in the commit messages) carry "columns a/b/c/d -> a/b/c/d" = charge review / claim inspector / unbilled / exclusions for EVERY slot; the Drive "_run report M-DD HHMM.csv" files only exist for some slots (9/28-10/3 this week, none for 10/4). Use the digests for the last run of each day and the Drive files as the cross-check.
- ERA auto-post e-mail ("Automation Center notification for ERA", noreply@advancedmd.com, ~04:15Z = the night of the previous ET day): one table per payment code (ACH / BOP / CHK / NON) with Date | Check Number | OK | Failed and a "N Completed ERA successfully posted; M Total" header. A check whose lines failed is RE-TRIED every night and re-counted (ACH 213453023 of 9/30: failed 21 -> 21 -> 19 -> 17 -> 17 as the 2-hourly runs posted lines by hand), so "latest night failed" is a stock, not a flow; summing nightly failed counts over a week double-counts.
- Scheduled cloud session (weekly task): no Claude-in-Chrome tools are present, so Report Center is always skipped in this task - every KEEP metric comes from the backend; the Report Center / XHR-capture leg only runs when William starts the task from Chrome.
- The weekly task's history sheet "Practice metrics - weekly" is re-created from CSV each run (create_file text/csv -> Sheet, then trash_file on the old id): 9/14 id 1HAxWX0f..., 9/21 id 1LgOu9SDMW6..., 10/5 id 1D1F_OwULk4_nSPT2zvr2l-zwB-aKxW1nkWlxOfSle7k. No 9/28 run happened, so the 10/5 deltas span two weeks.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-10-05 1510 UTC (11 AM ET 10/5 backend billing run, ehpa cloud)

## No new call shapes this run
Calls exercised (all in the registry): login_cached (ONE login 15:14Z, no lock), getclaimcenteroverview, getreviewcharges, getclaiminspectorbatches/getclaiminspectorcharges (batch 127783), getclaimunbillablecharges, getclaimrunstatus + getclaimexclusioncharges (81 runs, 0 rows), GET /api/era/checks (1 check), posterapayments --dry on 1052906 (0 postable / 17 skipped), gettxhistory visitid= x4 (reads only). No AMD writes.

## Lessons
- GITHUB PACKAGE TRANSPORT: a Drive 'github mirror' package part of 23,870 base64 chars re-typed from the Drive connector's download_file_content answer came back with the EXACT length but a wrong sha256, and `git index-pack` failed 'inflate: data stream error (incorrect data check) at offset 4036' - one or more flipped characters. Third failure of this route on the 10/5 packages (1110Z, 1310Z, 1510Z). The 9 KB pseudonyms.json came through intact the same way, so the practical cap for connector transcription is well under 20 KB; packages above that need a push from the session that wrote them (repo attached) or William's machine. Rule for the routine: try a package part once, verify with `git bundle verify` + `git fetch <bundle>` (index-pack validates the pack checksum even when no manifest sha is at hand), and leave it on the first failure.

# captured_calls additions - 2026-10-05 1710 UTC (1 PM ET 10/5 backend billing run, ehpa cloud)

## No new call shapes this run
Calls exercised (all in the registry): login_cached (ONE login 17:14Z, no lock), getclaimcenteroverview, getreviewcharges, getclaiminspectorbatches/getclaiminspectorcharges (batch 127783), getclaimunbillablecharges, getclaimrunstatus + getclaimexclusioncharges (81 runs, 1 row on 1700266), GET /api/era/checks (3 checks), GET /api/era/checkdetails (1052966, 1052967), posterapayments --dry on 1052906 (0 postable / 17 skipped) and LIVE on 1052966 (1 visit $156.51, HTTP 200), gettxhistory visitid= x4, getdemographic x2, checkEligibility (free 271) on pat17473087 + pat17472396 (--all), Stedi MBILUNOSSN (ERR 72) + insurance discovery on pat17473087.

## Lessons
- UHC SmartEdit UCMPI [Pattern 55252] 'this member has Medicare as their primary payor ... resubmit as electronic secondary once adjudicated by Medicare' arrives on the Exclusions column as a Change Health 'A7' acknowledgement ([name] FOR INVAL INFO + REJINFO 53845). It is a wrong-payer-order rejection: the cascade is AMD 271 on the UHC plan (it lists Medicare Part A/B as another payer but carries no MBI) -> Stedi MBI lookup -> discovery -> facesheet -> William. When no source yields the MBI the exclusion stays open (balance not $0, not re-sent) and the chart goes on William's turn for the Medicare card.
- Humana (MA) CO-B9 'patient is enrolled in a hospice' on visits a run had re-routed to the MA plan because the chart's Medicare plan was end-dated: the 9/20 hospice rule still applies (straight Medicare + GV, frequency 1) - the fix needs the Medicare plan's end date cleared (ins_update enddate='') before the visits can leave to Medicare, else they fall back into Unbilled 'DOS outside insurance coverage'.
- Auto-mode classifier refused even a --dry pass of act_1710_1005.py ('Stage 2 classifier error - blocking based on stage 1 assessment'); the denial applies to the outcome, so the live write was not attempted - approval package A-2026-10-05T1710Z-A1 per William's 9/30 rule.

## 10/6/2026 00:30-01:00Z - parse + plan lessons (same chat session)
- **LLM parse of a remit pile**: one background agent per scan batch writes `llm/<batch>_parse.json` (documents -> claims -> lines, with doc_type / family / method / card / correspondence). `billing/era_input/llm_merge.py` turns them into `remits.json` and merges with the regex pass (`remits_regex.json`). Rules learned tonight: the agent's `family` field is only a hint - agents wrote FLO99 for Florida Blue, MUT01 for Physicians Mutual and FCC/OTHER for Florida Community Care, so `fam_from_payer()` classifies from the payer NAME (PAYER_RULES, first match wins; TRICARE/Humana Military before HUMANA). Florida Blue is ALWAYS held (`is_florida_blue`): never promoted to the postable BLUE family even when a check is linked. A `vcard_stub` document that carries claims is a remittance paid by card (Aetna SS via Zelis, 17 claims $287.23 was being filed as a bare stub). When a regex record and an LLM record share (family, reference) the LLM record wins if it is valid and carries more claims or a different document amount (R020 ECHO $14.38/2 claims vs L007 $89.46/5 claims). One keying row per (card number, amount) - the same Zelis card can be scanned twice (stub page in one batch, EOB in another).
- **New postable family TRI00** (TRICARE East / Humana Military, carrier car6872): pays by paper check while the Optum ERA enrollment is in flight (voucher page 3 of 3, check 0061421001 $173.71). Added to post_paper CAR/CARCODE, build835 PAYERS (payer id 99727, PO Box 7981 Madison WI) + FILING "CH", plan.py POST_FAMILIES, llm_merge FAMILY_MAP.
- **CARC normaliser in build835 (`norm_cas`)**: payers print their own legend codes - FCC "PC 3", "PC 3/077", "PC 14/075" (contractual -> CO-45), Meritain "Provider Discount (QPA) / 890" (CO-45) and "Co-Ins" (PR-2), TRICARE patient cost share written as PR-45 (-> PR-2), CHAMPVA "OHI paid" text (OA-23), "services not covered" (CO-96). AMD's importer wants a numeric CARC per CAS; the built files now carry only CO-45/96/256/18, OA-23/253/18, PR-2.
- **Square reconciliation**: `payments.list` (Square connector, begin_time 9/1) - every card in the pile is matched by last4 + amount; 9/1-10/6 history shows only the 9/24 keying session (14 completed / 30 failed), so all 10 cards from this pile are still to key. The 9/24 failures are the cards the payers re-issued as paper checks in this pile (dedupe by (family, acct, paid) in plan.py keeps one posting).
- **Posting window**: the 2-hourly columns routine (trig_01QRqT8KB4Q1H9EyYRG4NBKX, :08 past odd UTC hours, ~30 min) also works the ERA Center - never upload synthetic 835s while a run is in flight (last_run.status PENDING); post in the gap, check `list_triggers` first.

## Lesson (helper, not a call): precheck.py carrier map shape
- bootstrap_workspace.sh layout derives carriers.json in the SIMPLE form (code -> carN) from practices/ehpa/carriers.json, while precheck.py built its code->carN map with `{v[0]: k ...}` from the RICH form (carN -> [code, name]). On the simple form v[0] is the letter 'c', so no carrier ever matched and every pair flagged 'no <carrier> plan on chart' (12 of 13 flags on 10/5 2310Z; the 9/18 '7 precheck plan-match misses' were the same defect). precheck.py now accepts both shapes. Re-run: 2 informational flags only.
- Still open in CAR2STEDI: FIR07 (no Stedi payer id mapped); FLO99 (FCC) has no Stedi eligibility - both report-only.

- LESSON (pat17475338 v19431100, 01:25Z): `move_to_ins.move(cid, seq="2,1")` used only to change a visit's insurance order ALSO calls `amd.rebill_charge(cid, <first seq of the new order>)` unless `rebill_seq=` is passed - on a visit already PAID by the first payer that queues a needless claim to it (here Medicare seq 2, before the frequency-7 flag was written for the secondary resend, so it will leave as frequency 7 with Florida Blue's ICN and should reject at the front end). For an order change on a paid visit pass `rebill_seq=<the sequence that must actually bill>` (or build the resave without the rebill). No captured AMD call un-queues a chargerebill once issued; the rejection is completed on Exclusions under the duplicate / invalid-reference rule.
- `lookupdiagcode class="lookup" exactmatch="0" code="M86.8%" orderby="code" page="1"` (and "M86.8X%", "M868X8%") returned an empty `<Results/>` at 01:18Z 10/6 although the 9/8 capture with code="M86.9%" worked - the code master's FY2027 state for M86.8X8 could not be read through this call; not re-probed (the FB letter was answered by deleting the code, M86.171 remained).
- Secondary corrected claim after the primary paid: dx edit via dx_primary_fix.save_visit_dx (full-visit echo + read-back), insorder "<primary>,<secondary>", then `freq7_visit.run(vid, cid, <secondary seq>, <secondary payer's ICN>)` -> 204; readback shows resubmissioncode="7" origrefnum="<ICN>" on the visit extins and the Medicare payment's own origrefnum untouched.
