## 2026-10-06T0330Z - chat (approved packages applied in ask mode) + the permanent fixes
- `updateinsurance` with `sequence="2,1"` (the PATIENT insurance order) sent through a plan that is END-DATED (pat12232248, ins6029665 just end-dated 06/21/2025) answers `success="1"` with `<patient insorder="" />` - the order is wiped, not set. The same call through the ACTIVE plan (ins9517757) landed `2,1`. `ins_update.update` now sends `sequence` as its own call through an active plan, verifies the answer (or a getdemographic re-read) and re-sends once through another active plan; a field edit and an order change are two calls.
- `chargerebill` twice for one visit: `move_to_ins.move(cid, seq)` rebills the first plan of the new order on its own, so `move_to_ins.move(cid, "1")` + `freq1_visit.run(vid, cid, 1)` (act_1710_1005, applied by the 10/5 2110Z run) is TWO rebills. Permanent guard: `chargerebill` rebills the WHOLE claim, so `amd.rebill_charge` sends a VISIT + sequence only once per script run - a repeat answers 204 "SKIPPED - already rebilled in this run" and sends nothing (`allow_repeat=True` = override; a 500/409 is never recorded); a later run is never blocked (a corrected rebill after a claim-run rejection still goes out); every 2xx rebill is appended to `registers/rebill_log.jsonl`. (A first design with a 6-hour cross-run ledger was dropped in review: it would have blocked the corrected rebill after a claim-run exclusion.) Re-route = `freq1_visit.reroute(vid, cid, order, seq)` (visit order by `resave.set_insorder`, a no-rebill full-visit echo, then ONE freq-1 reset + ONE rebill); `move_to_ins.move(cid, seq, rebill=False)` when a balance must move as well.
- `hideexclusions` run 1700265 visit 19455208 / claim 19455208B (Medigap secondary rejected 'subscriber not found' after the plan sat on UNI20): success="1" after the plan's carrier was moved to AAR01 (car6600).

# captured_calls additions - backend billing run 2026-10-06T1110Z (7 AM ET 10/6 slot, ehpa cloud)

No new AdvancedMD call SHAPES this run - every write went through proven shapes (hideexclusions, posterapayments, /api/era/checks/dismiss, void class=void, addpayments postingmethod=era). Helper patterns learned:

- **ERA Post button + CO-45 correction, the clean way (era_co45_relink.py, new 10/6/2026).** The Post button (posterapayments) posted Medicare ACH 819919368 (ERA 1053007, 20 claims / 24 lines, $1,953.55) with the CO45 / PR2 / CO253 reasons on every payment but payreasonwoamount 0 - the 9/23 defect, now seen on Medicare too: the contractual stayed on the INSURANCE balance on 23 charges (fee - paid, e.g. 74.54 on a $160 99233) and on the PATIENT balance on the one chart with no secondary. A bare manual_post write-off would have added a $0 code-less payment per line (-> Unbilled 'Payment reason codes' rows, the 9/21 / 10/6 0510Z lesson), so the correction is VOID the Post-button payment (void class=void <payment id=.../>) and RE-POST the same remit line ERA-linked (payreasons.post_line: addpayments postingmethod="era" eraclaimid/erareportid + the remit reasonlist + WOINS = CO+PI). Result per line: payment = remit paid with its reasons, WOINS = CO45+CO253, PR2 alone left (patient side on these Medicare-only visits). 24/24 lines corrected, WOINS $1,627.77. A line whose remainder sat on the PATIENT comes back only for the paid amount after the void (insurance balance = paid, so line_amounts caps the write-off at 0) - move_to_ins.move(cid) (no seq, no rebill) before the re-post (the helper does it since this run).
- **AMD removes a fully posted check from GET /api/era/checks on its own** (1053007 was gone right after the 20-visit Post; voiding + re-posting its lines did not bring it back). Only checks with unposted money or unprocessed $0 lines stay listed.
- **Payer duplicate-claim exclusions, generic helper (excl_dup_complete.py, new).** --scan <dir> --run <claimrunid> [--pid] [--live]: rows whose every error reads "DUPLICATE OF A PREVIOUSLY PROCESSED CLAIM/LINE" -> live tx read (records the payer's earlier answer on the visit) -> ONE hideexclusions per run -> re-list. Used on 5 HUM01 rows of run 1700266 (pat17472396, the 9/30 hospice GV re-route): Humana had already adjudicated them (a $0 COB9 NO-PAY on the 6/5 visit, 9/11/2026); completed, nothing re-sent; board row for William.
- **claim_status_sweep.py --skip-answered <claimstatus json>[,...]** (new option): visits an earlier sweep answered PAID / FINALIZED / DENIED / NOT ON FILE are not asked again, so each 2-hourly pass of step 5(b2) spends its 60 Stedi 277s on the next-largest balances (this run: 54 skipped, 60 new asked - 17 PAID-unposted $1,689.41 / $2,775.62 open, 31 DENIED 585 $5,210, 10 pending, 1 unknown, 1 D0 'date of service' on a 2023 visit that already carries a UHC ICN -> not sent). Register pattern: registers/uni20_bcbs_pre0501_sweep_<stamp>.json {summary, checks, rows}.
- **Medicare CO97 on 99344 for an established patient** (ERA 1053006 / 350041718, two $0 lines, RARC N211 MA15 N666): the new-patient home/ALF code billed days after paid 99233s of the same provider; standing recode 99344 -> 99349 (pcode65142). Medicare Part B takes no frequency 7 on professional claims, so the corrected claim is a new claim - on William's go (board rows), not sent by the run. Denial-only check dismissed with era_dismiss.py (its lines live in era_details/1053006.json).

# captured_calls additions - 2026-10-06 2310 UTC (7 PM ET backend billing run, ehpa cloud)

No new call shapes this run (ONE login 23:17Z; reads only: getclaimcenteroverview + the four list calls via columns_scan.py, GET /api/era/checks, GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid=1052906, massrebillsearch + 21 Stedi 277 claim-status checks via claim_status_sweep.py, precheck.py eligibility reads).

Lesson (TRAP, operational): `python3 era_post.py --help` is not a help flag - era_post.py parses sys.argv for --dry / --carrier / --visits / --all-unposted only, so any other flag runs a LIVE Post pass over the unposted list ("0 checks, dry=False" this run, nothing posted). Learn its options from the header or `grep sys.argv era_post.py`; never probe it with an unknown flag.

## 2026-10-07 11 AM ET run (2026-10-07T1510Z, ehpa cloud) - additions

No new call shapes this run. Calls used (all previously captured): login (one attempt, AMD_PSW env credential), getclaimcenteroverview + the four list calls (columns_scan.py before/after), GET /api/era/checks, GET /api/era/checkdetails (4 checks cached), POST /api/claim/massrebillsearch + Stedi claimstatus/v2 (claim_status_sweep.py, 21 x 277), gettxhistory visitid= (txh.fetch_visit), POST /api/era/checks/dismiss (check 1053025).

NEW HELPER: era_dismiss_zero.py <checkid> [--live] [--stamp] - dismisses ONE $0 ERA check whose every claim line is $0 paid, validated, and whose every named charge is at $0 insurance balance on the LIVE chart (gettxhistory visitid=); otherwise HOLD, nothing written. Register registers/era_dismiss_<stamp>[_dry].json. Fills the gap era_clear.py leaves on purpose ('denial with write-off (cascade handles)' holds a $0 check even after the cascade is done with it). Proven 10/7 1510Z on check 1053025 (WellCare 1003569030): HTTP 200 {"issuccess":true,"id":1053025,"actionname":1}, check gone from GET /api/era/checks.

claim_status_sweep.py --skip-answered takes a comma-separated list of the earlier sweep registers (registers/uni20_bcbs_pre0501_sweep_*.json, shape {about, stamp, rows}); the bare flag without a file list fails with IndexError (seen this run, re-run with the list).

# captured_calls additions - backend billing run 2026-10-07T1710Z (1 PM ET 10/7/2026, ehpa cloud)

No new AdvancedMD / Stedi call shapes this run. Calls exercised (all already in the registry): login (one), getclaimcenteroverview + the four Claims Center list calls (columns_scan.py before/after), GET /api/era/checks (6 -> 3 checks), GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid= (1053035, 1053036, 1053037 cached before posting), gettxhistory visitid= (live reads), updvisitwithcharges full-visit echo (move_to_ins.move, rebill=False, 9 visits), addpayments postingmethod=era (payreasons.post_line, 78 lines), POST /api/era/checks/dismiss x3.

- **ERA-linked addpayments accepts a carrier different from the check's system carrier (new 10/7/2026, run 2026-10-07T1710Z).** AdvancedMD filed two Florida Blue 835s (ACH 423816565 = ERA 1053037, $461.57; $0 remit 2610070965 = ERA 1053036) under FLORIDA MEDICARE (eracarrierid 1448 'FLORIDA BLUE' -> systemcarrierid 6085 FLO02) while 213469446 (ERA 1053035) landed correctly under BCBS 6062. The Post button would have recorded Medigap coinsurance as MEDICARE payments. era_new_1007_1710.py passes the check meta with systemcarrierid 6062 / systemcarriercode BCBS into payreasons.era_linked_xml (carrierid="car6062", eraclaimid / erareportid unchanged) - every line answered success="1" and the payment reads BCBS 423816565 on the chart (verified on patXXX v19436591 with --first-only before the batch). Rule: a remit whose eracarriername disagrees with systemcarriername is posted line by line with the override, never with the Post button; William fixes the ERA payer-id mapping (1448 -> Florida Blue) in Master Files.
- **Medigap coinsurance that AMD had moved to the PATIENT balance** (the Medicare Post button put PR-2 on the patient before the Medigap answered: chart 25596 x7, v19454644, v19447500): move_to_ins.move(cid, rebill=False) first (whole visit, no rebill), then the ERA-linked post - 9 visits, $199.21 moved and posted.
- **PR-1 deductible answer on an MA-primary claim** (v19456752, $0 paid, PR-1 230.44 + CO-45 202.56): ERA-linked post with pay 0 + WOINS of the CO-45 moves the PR amount to the patient balance by itself (after: insbalance 0, patbalance 144.96 / 85.48) - no separate transfer needed.
- **Dismiss gate for a hand-settled money check** (era_dismiss_settled_1007.py): GET /api/era/checkdetails only returns the UNPOSTED lines once a line is posted (the cached era_details/<checkid>.json shrinks to the leftovers on the next pull), so the gate reads 'every remaining line recorded by this run + unpostedamount 0' and then POST /api/era/checks/dismiss (JSON string id) -> {"issuccess":true,"id":<id>,"actionname":1}.

# captured_calls additions - backend billing run 2026-10-07T1910Z (3 PM ET 10/7/2026, ehpa cloud)

No new AdvancedMD / Stedi call shapes this run. Calls exercised (all already in the registry): login (one), getclaimcenteroverview + the four Claims Center list calls (columns_scan.py before/after), gettxhistory (admission anchors + live reads), updextrainsinfo hospitalizationfrom, updvisitwithcharges full-visit echo (resave onhcfa=1; move_to_ins no-rebill), getclaiminspectorcharges re-list, GET /api/era/checks (4 -> 3), GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid=1053038, addpayments postingmethod=era (payreasons.post_line, 41 lines), updextrainsinfo resubmissioncode=1 + POST /api/claim/chargerebill (freq1_visit.run, 204), hideexclusions (6 rows, run 1700276), POST /api/era/checks/dismiss (1053038), getdemographic (plan reads).

- **A timely-filing write-off goes through the ERA-linked shape unchanged (10/7/2026, run 2026-10-07T1910Z).** payreasons.post_line(pid, vid, chk, v, cid, ch, pay=0, wo=<open balance>, wocode="WOLATE") with the remit's own CO-29 reason in the reasonlist answered success="1" on all 40 lines (patXXX x34, patXXX x4, patXXX x2); the charge reads insbalance 0 / patbalance 0 afterwards and the payment carries the CO29 reason, so the visit never lands in Unbilled 'Payment reason codes are missing'. Same shape as the WOINS contractual post; only the write-off code differs.
- **Carrier override for a UHC remit AMD files under UNI20 while the chart's plan is UHCMA (Dual Complete):** the check meta handed to payreasons.era_linked_xml with systemcarrierid 302853 / systemcarriercode UHCMA posts the payment under the billed carrier (1 line, success="1") - the 1710Z Florida-Blue-under-Medicare lesson generalises to any remit whose system carrier differs from the live charge's carrier.
- **UHC SmartEdit duplicate rows carry four error lines** (REJINFO 'missing or invalid information' + P4999UDCP3 'duplicate of previously submitted claim id [FX...] received [date]'); excl_dup_complete.py's all-lines-duplicate filter misses them - excl_dup_complete_1910.py matches any line (DUPLICATE OF (A )?PREVIOUSLY (PROCESSED|SUBMITTED) CLAIM) on a listed visit set and completes them with hideexclusions (success 1, re-list 0).
# captured_calls additions - backend billing run 2026-10-07T2110Z (5 PM ET 10/7/2026, ehpa cloud)

No new AdvancedMD / Stedi call shapes this run. Calls exercised (all already in the registry): login (one), getclaimcenteroverview + the four Claims Center list calls (columns_scan.py before/after), gettxhistory visitid= (live reads, txh.fetch_visit), GET /api/era/checks (6 -> 3 -> 1), GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid= (1053024, 1053040-1053046), addpayments postingmethod=era (payreasons.post_line, 31 lines: 23 payments, 5 WOINS-only / WOCAID2ND-only $0 posts under Sunshine and Humana, 0 faults), POST /api/era/checks/dismiss (1053040, 1053044, 1053024), POST /api/claim/massrebillsearch + Stedi 276/277 (claim_status_sweep.py pass 16, 21 claims), getdemographic (plan reads).

- **An ERA line with chargedetailid -1 on a two-charge CCM visit is matched to the live charge by CPT + DOS (era_new_1007_2110.py NEW C) - and when the match finds nothing, read the visit before calling it an orphan.** On 1053040 (AARP Medigap, check 00148133556) the two unmatched 99489 lines (pat17476032 v19452333 $8.92, pat45108 v19452606 $15.49) belong to add-on charges that were VOIDED on 9/1/2026 (gettxhistory shows the 99489 with void="1" and a -109.00 reversal charge with no bill date): the payer paid a line AMD no longer has. Recorded as orphan / residual money in the run register, nothing posted, no board row (ERA step 5(d)).
- **A check whose every line gets a posting leaves GET /api/era/checks on its own - no dismiss call needed.** 1053041 (Sunshine $0, three WOCAID2ND posts), 1053042 (Humana, one pay + WOINS), 1053045 (Humana NO-PAY, three WOCAID2ND) and 1053046 (Ambetter, one pay + WOINS) were "not on the list" seconds after the ERA-linked posts; era_dismiss_settled_2110.py says "not on the list" and writes nothing. Dismiss is only for a check that keeps $0 HOLD / recorded lines or residual money lines (1053040 with 2 residual + 2 orphan lines, 1053044 with 2 HOLD lines, 1053024 record-only) - each answered {"issuccess":true,"id":<checkid>,"actionname":1}.
- **The carrier override of the 1710Z / 1910Z lessons is for the SAME payer filed under another AMD carrier record only** (UHC family UNI20 / UHCMA / AAR01; a Florida Blue 835 AMD files under FLORIDA MEDICARE). A Humana remit answering on a visit whose live claim is at Medicare (the 10/5 hospice GV re-send of pat17472396) posts under HUMANA, not under the live carrier - era_new_1007_2110.py restricts the override to an alias table (alias_ok) and HOLDS a $0 answer whose live claim sits at a different payer (the old plan's answer: v19441616 CO-45 + PR-1, v19442523's 99489 CO-4 - listed on the board row, nothing written).
- **A Medicaid-type secondary's $0 OA-23 answer on a line another payer already paid is the dual crossover residual -> WOCAID2ND of the open balance through the same ERA-linked shape** (payreasons.post_line pay 0, wo = open balance, wocode WOCAID2ND, the remit's OA23/CO45 reasons kept): 3 x 21.80 Sunshine (X319751693) on pat17472546, 3 x 16.14 Humana NO-PAY (202610060006487) on pat17471238 - success="1" each, balances 0, no 'Payment reason codes' Unbilled row.


# captured_calls additions - backend billing run 2026-10-07T2310Z (7 PM ET 10/7/2026, ehpa cloud)

Calls exercised (already in the registry): login (one, 23:14Z), getclaimcenteroverview + the four Claims Center list calls (columns_scan.py before/after), gettxhistory visitid= (txh.fetch_visit live reads), GET /api/era/checks (2 -> 1), GET /api/era/checkdetails?checkid= (1052906, 1053047), addpayments postingmethod=era (payreasons.post_line: 3 payments + 5 WOLATE-only lines on UHC W0904074, 0 faults), void class=void (void_tx.void_writeoff, 1 write-off), POST /api/era/checks/dismiss (1053047), checkEligibility (elig_amd.py pat17473233), Stedi MBILUNOSSN + insurance discovery (pat17473233 at DOS 1/27/2026), POST /api/claim/massrebillsearch + Stedi 276/277 (claim_status_sweep.py pass 17).

- **NEW SHAPE - write-off-only Payment Entry (addpayments with an EMPTY <paymentlist/>): a contractual write-off with no $0 payment line.** `<ppmdmsg action="addpayments" class="paymententry" ... nocookie="1"><patient patientid="patN" paysource="2" postingmethod="" amount="0.00" paycode="PE" woamount="<wo>" paymethod="9" carrierid="<carN number>" depositdate="mm/dd/yyyy" case_note="..." checknumber="<original check>" inclstmt="0" postedby="POI0712" batch=""><unappliedpaymentlist/><unappliedwriteofflist/><chargelist><charge id="chargeN" allowed="0.00" insportion=".." patportion=".." insbalance="<bal>" patbalance=".." profile="<visit profile>"><paymentlist/><writeofflist><writeoff id="" risk="0" amount="<wo>" code="WOINS" status="B"/></writeofflist></charge></chargelist></patient></ppmdmsg>` -> success="1", the charge's insurance balance drops by <wo>, and NO $0 code-less payment is born (manual_post.post with pay 0 always writes `<payment amount="0.00" .../>`, the line that lands a visit in Unbilled 'Payment reason codes are missing or do not balance' when a next-payer claim is built). Proven 10/7/2026 23:3xZ on pat17475777 v19435223 (charges 23342218 WOINS 93.81, 23342219 WOINS 80.66 under the original UHC check V5475692 - the OA-18 duplicate rule: settle the original adjudication's contractual, never rebill). Helper era_1053047_fix_2310.py section B (manual_post fallback kept, not needed). Use it for every bare write-off from now on (paper-EOB write-offs, duplicate-original contractuals, residual adjustments) - the ERA-linked shape stays the rule for a line that has its 835 in the ERA Center.
- **A remit that pays a line the chart had written off WOINS after an earlier payer's $0 answer: void the write-off (void class=void writeoff id), re-read, then post the money ERA-linked.** pat17476748 v19453702 99349 9/2/2026: Medicare's $0 of 9/10 had been posted with WOINS 198.00 (write-off 22480287) before the claim went on to UHC; UHC W0904074 paid 104.25 (CO-45 91.63 + CO-253 2.12). void_writeoff -> success="1" (insurance balance 198.00 back, nothing on the patient), payreasons.post_line pay 104.25 + WOINS 93.75 -> success="1", balance 0. era_new_1007_2310.py had called it 'residual 104.25' (balance 0) - a residual is only a residual when another payer's MONEY sits on the line; a write-off is reversible (era_1053047_fix_2310.py section A).
- **PR-26 'expenses incurred prior to coverage' from UHC on a 1/27/2026 CCM (pat17473233 v19430640, $206):** the Stedi CMS 271 at that DOS names CAPITAL HEALTH PLAN as the HMO-Medicare-Risk payer 1/1-2/28/2026 (UHC's AARP MA only from 3/1/2026); Stedi discovery returns no CHP member id and the chart has none -> board row on William's turn for the CHP card / member id, nothing re-sent (a UNI20 pre-5/1 visit whose payer on the DOS was another plan is never sent to UNI20 - v12 STANDING FLAGS).
- **CO-29 on UHC W0904074 (ERA 1053047) on five 2025 UNI20-primary lines of pat24655 ($828) written off WOLATE through the ERA-linked shape** (the 1910Z recipe, era_new_1007_2310.py) and recorded as tf_writeoff_watch.json item uhc-co29-answers-2026-10-07T2310Z (committed alone on main). The check's 25 other $0 lines were already at $0 (the 1910Z WOLATE pass, OA-18 duplicates of the 9/6-9/10 sends); two CO-B9 hospice answers on pat17473477 (v19442357, v19448745) are the old plan's answers on claims re-routed to Medicare 10/2 (HELD, listed); the OA-18 on pat17473477 v19420969 duplicates UHC's CO-151 of ERA 1052921 (the CCM cluster on the board).
